### [194] [Evidence-Supported Credit Risk Report Generation Using News-Centric Financial Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01023)
*Rocio Jimenez-Villen,Ziwei Xu,Ying Chen,Oscar Araque,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinKG-News框架，通过构建以新闻事件为锚点、链接公司的事件-市场知识图谱，结合情境学习架构生成信贷风险报告，在质量提升19%-34%的同时减少幻觉，并发现自动化幻觉检测和质量评估仍不可靠，需专家判断辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场受新闻事件驱动，但现有方法未能显式建模事件与市场之间的关系，导致市场动态解释不充分。

Method: 提出FinKG-News框架，自动构建以新闻事件为锚点、链接公司的事件-市场知识图谱；并基于此开发情境学习架构，用于信贷风险报告生成。

Result: 相比基线方法，质量提升19%-34%，幻觉显著减少；自动评估和人工评估均表明，现有自动化幻觉检测和质量评估方法不可靠，专家判断不可或缺。

Conclusion: FinKG-News通过显式建模事件-市场关系有效提升了金融报告生成质量并减少了幻觉，但自动化评估手段仍需改进，专家判断在当前阶段仍不可替代。

Abstract: Financial markets evolve in response to real-world events reported in news, yet these drivers often remain implicit in text. To better explain market dynamics, event-market relations must be explicitly modeled through factual, company-centric, and environment-aware knowledge graphs. We present FinKG-News, a framework that automatically constructs such graphs by extracting news events as anchors linked to companies. Using FinKG-News as grounded evidence that integrates events, news, and company data, we develop an in-context learning architecture for credit risk report generation across three core financial dimensions. Automatic and human evaluations show that automated hallucination detection and quality assessment remain unreliable, making expert judgment indispensable. Our approach consistently outperforms baselines, improving quality by 19%-34% while reducing hallucinations. The source code and project resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/ichise-laboratory/FINKG-news.

</details>


### [195] [Conversable Complexity: Agentic LLM Collectives as Interpretable Substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01047)
*Elias Najarro,Ane Espeseth,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体群体作为人工生命（ALife）研究的计算基底，这些智能体通过自然语言交互展现涌现动态，并因其语言特性而具备天然的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂性与可解释性通常难以兼得：足够复杂的系统往往不透明，而透明的系统又过于简单。本文旨在弥合这一矛盾，利用LLM智能体群体既复杂又可通过自然语言直接探查的特点，为人工生命研究提供新的计算基底。

Method: 将LLM改造为具有持久记忆、工具使用、共享技能和主动发起行动能力的智能体（agentic LLMs），并将多个这样的智能体组成群体，利用其自然语言交互产生的文本痕迹进行行为分析和解释。

Result: 发现LLM群体表现出单个孤立模型所不具有的涌现动态。通过自然语言交互，可以直观地审视和询问智能体群体行为背后的逻辑，实现了复杂系统的一定程度可解释性。

Conclusion: LLM智能体群体作为人工生命研究的计算基底是可行的，其自然语言特性为复杂涌现系统的可解释性提供了新途径。文中还综述了从受控实验到实际部署中的相关实例。

Abstract: Complexity and interpretability rarely coincide: systems rich enough for complex behaviours to emerge are usually too opaque to question, while transparent ones are too simple for anything complex to emerge. A single large language model (LLM) is a static artefact, hardly exhibiting any of the emergent properties we associate with life. This changes through interaction: populations of LLMs display emergent dynamics absent from isolated models. Furthermore, LLMs can be endowed with persistent memory, tools and shared skills, and the capacity to initiate actions unprompted, i.e., turning LLMs agentic. In this paper, we argue that such collectives of agents can serve as a computational substrate for Artificial Life (ALife) research. Critically, since the agents communicate in natural language, their collective behaviour can be directly interrogated by examining textual traces and asking the agents themselves. We outline the notion of interpretability in language-model research and extend it for collectives of agents. Lastly, we survey recent examples of agentic LLM collectives that already instantiate the idea of agentic substrates, from controlled experiments to deployments in the wild.

</details>


### [196] [Message Passing Enables Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01077)
*Xuecheng Liu,Daman Arora,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出消息传递语言模型（MPLMs）框架，允许LLM推理线程通过轻量级发送/接收原语直接通信，通过减少冗余上下文共享和抢占机制实现高效并行扩展。在数独、3-SAT和长上下文问答任务上验证了其相比串行CoT和并行FJ方法的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行扩展方法（如fork-join）中线程是瞬态的且无法进行点对点通信，限制了可扩展性。为克服这一瓶颈，需要一种允许线程直接通信的框架。

Method: 提出MPLM框架，核心包括两个机制：(1) 通过避免冗余上下文共享降低通信成本；(2) 抢占机制允许线程基于来自同伴的部分信息提前终止。在数独任务上使用微调模型，在3-SAT上利用抢占剪枝，在长上下文QA中使用大型预训练模型通过提示遵循MPLM协议。

Result: 在数独上，MPLM渐近地需要比串行CoT和并行FJ更小的上下文，并能解决25x25规模难题（对标准CoT/FJ和前沿推理模型仍具挑战）。在3-SAT上，抢占机制提高了效率。在长上下文QA上，通过提示达到与流行fork-join方法竞争的结果。

Conclusion: MPLM通过线程间直接通信和抢占机制，在多种推理任务上实现了比串行CoT和并行FJ更高效和可扩展的推理，证明了消息传递范式在LLM推理中的潜力。

Abstract: While inference-time scaling has improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), the need to generate long chains-of-thought (CoTs) is a computational bottleneck. Thus, in contrast to sequential scaling methods like CoT, recent parallel scaling techniques instead use fork and join (FJ) primitives to divide work across multiple LLM threads. However, in the fork-join paradigm, threads are typically transient and do not communicate pointwise with one another which limits scalability. To tackle this, we introduce Message Passing Language Models (MPLMs), a framework for LLM reasoning in which threads communicate directly via lightweight send and receive primitives. MPLMs enable efficient scaling through two key mechanisms: (1) reduced communication costs, achieved by avoiding redundant context sharing, and (2) preemption, which allows threads to terminate early based on partial information from their peers.
  We demonstrate the promise of MPLMs on 3 classes of tasks. First, on Sudoku puzzles, we show that MPLMs require an asymptotically smaller context than both serial CoT and parallel FJ. We then fine-tune a single model to solve 25 x 25 puzzles that remain challenging for standard CoT and FJ approaches, as well as frontier reasoning models without tools. Second, on 3-SAT puzzles, the capability of preemption allows termination of unpromising branches, which results in improved efficiency. Finally, we show that appropriately prompted large pre-trained models follow the MPLM protocol, achieving competitive results on long-context question answering relative to popular fork-join approaches.

</details>


### [197] [Clinician-Level Agreement Without Clinical Caution: LLM Evaluator Limits in Medical AI Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01103)
*William Philipp,Finn Fassbender,Thorsten Langer,Martje Pauly,Rebecca Herzog,Alexander Baumann,Markus Hobert,Theresa Paulus,Ip Chi Wang,Lukas Goede,Johanna Reimer,Sebastian Löns,Ronald Böck,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedQADE是首个德语临床开放问答基准，包含3,800个项目由十位医生和九个LLM评估器标注。最佳评估器Gemini 3 Flash与医生的校准一致性达到统计对齐（κ=0.694 vs. κ=0.709），但自动评估器缺乏临床元认知（医生会根据难度弃权，而模型对所有项目给出确定分数），且存在系统性血缘依赖偏差（模型偏好架构相似的模型），表明统计对齐不能保证临床谨慎性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放问答评估相比多项选择基准具有更强的临床有效性，但评分存在瓶颈，促使使用LLM作为自动评估器。然而，这类评估器能否复制临床校准和谨慎性尚未经过检验，特别是在德语这一缺乏原生评估基础设施的主要临床语言中。

Method: 构建MedQADE基准，包含3,800个开放问答项目，由十位执业医师和九个LLM评估器进行标注。比较评估器与医生的一致性（κ统计量），分析自动评估器的临床元认知（弃权行为与题目难度的关系），并通过跨模型评估量化血缘依赖偏差。

Result: 最佳评估器Gemini 3 Flash与医生达到统计对齐（κ=0.694），接近医生间一致性（κ=0.709），但置信区间较宽。自动评估器几乎完全缺乏临床元认知（医生随难度增加而弃权，模型始终给出确定分数）。存在系统性血缘依赖偏差，模型倾向于给架构相似的模型更高分数，且该偏差独立于语言。

Conclusion: 统计对齐并不能确保临床谨慎性，评估器独立性需要明确验证。自动评估器可能在统计上匹配医生判断，但缺乏临床元认知和存在血缘偏差可能削弱其实际临床可用性。

Abstract: Open-response evaluation provides stronger clinical validity than multiple-choice benchmarks but creates a scoring bottleneck that motivates automated LLM-asa-Judge approaches. Whether such evaluators replicate clinical calibration and caution, however, remains untested. We introduce MedQADE, the first standardised open-response clinical benchmark for German, a major clinical language lacking native evaluation infrastructure, comprising 3,800 items annotated by ten practising physicians and nine Large Language Model (LLM) evaluators. The top-performing evaluator model, Gemini 3 Flash, reached alignment consistent with the physician ceiling (\k{appa} = 0.694 vs. \k{appa} = 0.709), though wide confidence intervals limit interpretation. Despite this statistical alignment, automated evaluators exhibited near-absent clinical metacognition: physicians scaled abstention with item difficulty, while frontier models assigned definitive scores in every case. We additionally quantified systematic lineage-dependent biases, where models preferentially scored architectural siblings, an effect independent of language. These results show that statistical alignment does not ensure clinical caution, and that evaluator independence requires explicit verification.

</details>


### [198] [Towards Developing a Multimodal Chat Assistant for University Stakeholders: RAG-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01115)
*Md Abu Hanif Shaikh,Abdullah Al Shafi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态大学聊天机器人系统，结合大语言模型与检索增强生成（RAG）技术，能够处理文本和图像查询，并将幻觉率从31.7%降低至6.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大学利益相关者难以获取及时可靠的信息，尤其是在发展中国家，缺乏智能支持系统；现有的基于规则的聊天机器人无法处理复杂的领域特定查询，也难以适应不断变化的机构政策。

Method: 系统将大语言模型与语义检索相结合（RAG），从大学手册等机构资源中生成基于上下文的响应；通过视觉语言模型接受文本和图像查询；应用量化推理以在受限硬件上快速部署；后端使用FastAPI构建，前端使用Next.js开发。

Result: 多模态评估显示系统在文本和图像查询上均保持较高的满意度；定量评估表明，幻觉率从31.7%降至6.6%，验证了检索增强的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态RAG聊天机器人系统有效降低了幻觉，能够处理文本和图像查询，为大学场景提供了可靠的信息支持方案。

Abstract: University stakeholders often face difficulties in accessing timely and reliable information, especially in developing countries, where there are very few intelligent support systems. Existing rule-based chatbots are unable to handle complex, domain-specific queries and are not well-equipped to adapt to evolving institutional policies. As a fill-in-the-gap solution, we present the multimodal university chatbot with retrieval-augmented generation. The system combines the large language model with semantic retrieval to produce context-based responses from institution-centric resources, such as the university handbook. The system accepts text and image queries through the vision-language model and applies quantized inference for rapid deployment on constrained hardware. A scalable backend built with FastAPI, adjoined with a responsive frontend developed with Next.js, ensures real-time usability. Our multimodal evaluation demonstrates that the system maintains strong satisfaction scores across both text and image queries, despite increased response time for visual inputs. Furthermore, quantitative evaluation shows that hallucination is reduced from 31.7% to 6.6% in our proposed RAG-based system, confirming the effectiveness of retrieval grounding.

</details>


### [199] [$\text{Log}_\text{b}$Quant: Quantizing Language Models in Logarithmic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01127)
*Jeremias Bohn,Tizian Dippold,Mahdi Koubaa,Elias R. Wahl,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LogₐQuant，一种可调节基数的对数量化方法，在4位精度下相比非对称线性量化在性能上表现更优，同时实现适度的速度提升和高内存节省，适用于消费级GPU的私有部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的均匀量化方法在处理低频高幅值权重时容易产生次优表示，这限制了量化在语言模型压缩中的效果。

Method: 提出LogₐQuant，一种基于可调节基数的对数量化方法，以适应常见的参数分布。

Result: 在4位精度下，基于张量粒度的LogₐQuant在多个性能基准上优于非对称线性量化，同时实现适度的加速和高内存节省。

Conclusion: LogₐQuant适合在消费级GPU上进行私有化部署，兼顾性能、速度和内存效率。

Abstract: Quantization has become an invaluable tool to reduce memory requirements and inference speed of modern language models, in particular to make them available for consumer setups and edge devices. While previous work has primarily focused on uniform quantization codebooks, such approaches are prone to suboptimal representations due to low-frequency high-magnitude weights. We introduce Log$_\text{b}$Quant, a novel logarithmic quantization approach with adjustable bases, to adapt to common parameter distributions. We show that our method exhibits superior performance at 4-bit precision on several performance benchmarks compared to asymmetric linear quantization at tensor-wise granularity, while achieving moderate speedup and high memory savings, making it suitable for private use on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [200] [AGC-Bench: Measuring Artificial General Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01152)
*Roger Beaty,Vijeta Deshpande,Clin K. Y. Lai,Anna Attuch,Namrata Shivagunde,Swastik Roy,Rajkumar Pujari,Paul V. DiStefano,Sherin Muckatira,Claire E. Stevenson,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGC-Bench，一个系统构建的人工通用创造力基准，包含78个数据集覆盖六大创造力领域。通过因子分析发现LLM存在类似智力"g"因子的创造力"c"因子（解释81.5%方差），且与通用知识/推理可分离。还开发了基于项目反应理论校准的AGC-Judge评估模型，实验表明"发挥创意"提示比推理提示更能提升创造力表现，但人类顶尖水平仍领先LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造力研究长期争论创造力是领域特异性的还是可分离于通用智力，这些问题现在同样适用于LLM，但缺乏统一的人工智能创造力基准。

Method: 系统综述3101篇论文筛选497个基准构建AGC-Bench，覆盖78个数据集和六大领域；应用Judge Response Theory（项目反应理论）校准LLM评判者的宽严偏差，微调Qwen3-30B得到AGC-Judge；对83个LLM进行因子分析。

Result: 因子分析发现单一创造力因子"c"解释81.5%方差，与通用知识/推理相关但可分离；"发挥创意"提示比推理提示更能提升表现；人类顶尖水平仍领先LLM。

Conclusion: AGC-Bench作为开放基础设施可大规模测量AI创造力，发现LLM创造力存在类似智力的单一因子结构，且与通用能力可分离，但人类在创造力上仍具优势。

Abstract: Creativity research has debated whether creativity is domain-specific (e.g., visual, writing, science), and if it is psychometrically separable from general intelligence. Both questions now apply to LLMs, but a unified benchmark of AI creativity remains elusive. We introduce AGC-Bench, an artificial general creativity benchmark built from a systematic review of the AI creativity literature (3,101 papers screened, 497 benchmarks identified), paired with an agentic harness that converts idiosyncratic codebases into HELM-standardized benchmarks. The first release covers 78 datasets spanning brainstorming, problem solving, STEM, narrative, figurative language, and humor. To address bias in LLM-as-judge, we apply Judge Response Theory -- a psychometric calibration of judge leniency/severity; we then fine-tune Qwen3-30B on the bias-corrected ratings of three frontier LLMs to produce AGC-Judge, an open-weight model that robustly scores new creativity benchmarks it was not trained on. Results reveal frontier models at the top of the AGC-Bench leaderboard, with open models close behind. LLMs show different creative strengths, ranking higher on some domains (e.g., writing) than others (e.g., scientific ideation). Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, applying factor analysis across 83 LLMs, we recover a single creativity factor 'c', analogous to the 'g' factor of general intelligence, that explains 81.5% of variance, related to but separable from general knowledge/reasoning. Second, we show that prompting models to "be creative" boosts their performance far more than enabling reasoning, evidence that the benchmark tracks creativity over general ability. Third, on a human-matched subset, we find the top human still leads the top LLM on creativity. We release AGC-Bench with a public leaderboard, AGC-Judge, and human data as open infrastructure for measuring AI creativity at scale.

</details>


### [201] [Adversarial Pragmatics for AI Safety Evaluation: A Benchmark for Instruction Conflict, Embedded Commands, and Policy Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01153)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"对抗语用学"作为评估语言模型在指令冲突、嵌入命令、引用、范围歧义、指示语、间接言语行为和多轮智能体交互等复杂语言现象下行为的基准和注释协议，提供了系统的分类学、种子基准集和评估指标，将语言学判断方法转化为验证安全评估和LLM评判器的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估多将模型行为压缩为二元通过/失败标签，无法区分失败源于能力限制、策略歧义、指令冲突、支架失败还是评估者判断不稳定，缺乏对自然语言歧义性行为的细粒度分析方法。

Method: 构建了一个语言学控制的分类体系，开发了18个项目的种子基准集（含验证器强制的元数据）、54行的本地种子试点、专家评估协议（区分任务成功、策略合规、安全风险、拒绝结果和评估者置信度），以及评判者有效性、诊断歧义性和分类漂移等指标。

Result: 提供了一个将语言学判断方法论转化为验证安全评估、LLM评判器、黄金集构建、提示注入测试和安全文档的实用框架，并配套了相关的评估指标。

Conclusion: 对抗语用学框架能够系统性地处理语言歧义带来的评估挑战，为安全评估提供了更精细、更可靠的方法论基础。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for language models increasingly depend on judgments about ambiguous natural-language behaviour: whether a model has followed an instruction, refused appropriately, complied with a policy, resisted an embedded command, or misreported progress in an agentic task. Existing benchmarks often compress these distinctions into pass/fail labels, obscuring whether failures arise from capability limits, policy ambiguity, instruction conflict, scaffold failure, or unstable evaluator judgments.
  This paper introduces adversarial pragmatics as a benchmark and annotation protocol for evaluating model behaviour under instruction conflict, embedded commands, quotation, scope ambiguity, deixis, indirect speech acts, and multi-turn agent transcripts. The contribution is empirical and methodological: a linguistically controlled taxonomy, an 18-item seed benchmark with validator-enforced metadata, a 54-row local seed pilot, an expert-evaluation protocol distinguishing task success, policy compliance, safety risk, refusal outcome, and evaluator confidence, and metrics for judge validity, diagnostic ambiguity, and taxonomy drift. The framework turns linguistic judgment methodology into a practical tool for validating safety evals, LLM judges, gold-set construction, prompt-injection tests, and safety documentation.

</details>


### [202] [Distill to Detect: Exposing Stealth Biases in LLMs through Cartridge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01208)
*Shayan Talaei,Abhinav Chinta,Devvrit Khatri,Amin Karbasi,Azalia Mirhoseini,Amin Saberi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 Distill to Detect (D2D) 的方法，通过将可疑模型与基座模型之间的分布差异蒸馏到 KV-cache 前缀适配器中，放大隐蔽偏好偏见的信号，使其在生成文本中可被检测，从而解决部署语言模型中隐形偏好偏见的检测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在关键场景中可能隐含对特定实体、品牌或观点偏好，这些偏好由供应链中任意方引入，且最危险的是模型仅在相关话题上表现出偏好，而在其他输入上保持与基座模型一致。现有方法无法在未知偏见主题的情况下可靠检测这种隐蔽偏好偏见，存在防御方的不对称困境。

Method: D2D 方法将可疑模型与基座模型之间的 logit 分布差异，通过上下文蒸馏的方式浓缩到一个轻量级 KV-cache 前缀适配器（称为 cartridge）中，从而放大主导差异并将偏见信号转化为可观测的生成文本。理论框架通过 Fisher 加权投影解释分布偏移的放大机制。

Result: 实验表明 D2D 能够在多种偏见类型上成功放大隐蔽模型的隐藏偏见，使其达到可被可靠检测的程度。理论分析通过 Fisher 加权投影视角得到经验支持。

Conclusion: D2D 将前缀适配器的容量瓶颈转化为检测工具，为审计部署语言模型中的隐藏行为提供了实用的基础组件，有效打破了防御方在未知偏见主题下的检测不对称性。

Abstract: Language models deployed in high-stakes roles can potentially favor certain entities, brands, or viewpoints, steering user decisions at scale. Such preferential biases can be introduced by any actor in the model's supply chain and are most dangerous when the model reveals its preference only on the relevant topic while behaving identically to its unmodified base on all other inputs. Recent work has shown that these biases can transfer through context distillation on semantically unrelated data, with the signal residing entirely in the soft logit distribution and remaining invisible to text-based inspection. However, the defender faces a fundamental asymmetry: without knowing the bias topic, no detection method can reliably surface a stealth preferential bias, regardless of whether it examines generated text, internal representations, or model weights. Here we introduce Distill to Detect (D2D), a method that surfaces hidden biases by distilling the distributional shift between a suspected model and its base into a cartridge (a KV-cache prefix adapter), concentrating the dominant divergence and amplifying the bias signal into generated text. We show that D2D successfully amplifies the hidden biases of stealth models to the extent that they can be reliably detected across multiple bias types. We also propose a theoretical framework that explains the efficacy of D2D through the lens of Fisher-weighted projection of the logit distribution shift, supported by empirical observations. By turning the capacity bottleneck of prefix-tuning adapters into a detection tool, D2D provides a practical building block for auditing hidden behaviors in deployed language models.

</details>


### [203] [The State-Prediction Separation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01218)
*Giovanni Monea,Nathan Godey,Kianté Brantley,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出状态-预测分离假说，通过设计双计算流Transformer解耦下一token预测与状态存储功能，实验证明该方法在多个规模上能提升数据与计算效率，并在下游任务上平均提升2-3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer使用同一前向计算流同时进行下一token预测和状态存储，这两种角色的混合可能限制了语言模型的性能。作者提出状态-预测分离假说，旨在通过解耦这两个功能来提升语言建模性能。

Method: 设计了双计算流的Transformer变体，将下一token预测和状态存储分别分配到两个独立的计算流中，并进行多规模的预训练实验。

Result: 状态-预测分离在多个规模上一致地提升了数据效率和计算效率，改进了验证损失，在下游任务上平均比标准Transformer高出2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过大量实证分析排除了潜在混淆因素，证明了所提设计在梯度上的根本性差异，验证了状态-预测分离假说对语言建模性能提升的有效性。

Abstract: Transformers use the same forward computation stream to both predict the next token and store useful state for future token predictions. We formulate the \emph{state-prediction separation hypothesis}: disentangling the two roles yields better language modeling performance. We design a Transformer variant that uses two computation streams to separate the two functions, and conduct pretraining experiments across various scales. Our experiments show that state-prediction separation consistently offers better data and compute efficiencies, improving validation loss and outperforming standard Transformers by 2--3 percentage points on average on downstream tasks. We also conduct extensive empirical analysis that rules out potential confounders and demonstrates the fundamental difference in the gradients our design entails.

</details>


### [204] [Measuring the Gap Between Human and LLM Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01233)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建大规模评估框架，通过逆向工程从高质量论文中提取关键参考文献，让LLM基于这些文献生成新想法，并引入双轴研究品味分类法分析发现：LLM生成的想法集中在桥梁式机会和综合方法上，而人类研究想法分布更广泛，表明LLM的研究品味与人类存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大多从新颖性、可行性或专家偏好角度评判LLM生成的单个研究想法，缺乏对LLM想法与人类研究者之间整体差距的系统性量化分析。

Method: 构建大规模评估框架：1）对每篇高质量论文逆向工程出其核心创意可能受启发的少量相关先前工作；2）让LLM基于这些论文标题和摘要生成新想法；3）提出双轴研究品味分类法（机会模式与研究范式），量化人类与LLM想法的分布差异。

Result: 不同LLM生成的想法集呈现一致的分布差距：LLM想法不成比例地集中在桥梁式机会和综合方法上，而人类论文参考分布更广泛地覆盖了构建研究空白和贡献的各种方式。

Conclusion: 强LLM能产生一系列合理想法，但这一想法的范围比人类研究品味更窄且存在系统性偏移。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to brainstorm research ideas, but existing evaluations mostly judge individual ideas by novelty, feasibility, or expert preference. We instead ask: how far are current LLM-generated ideas from human researchers? To characterize this gap, we build a large-scale evaluation framework for ideation from high-quality human research papers. For each paper, we reverse-engineer a small set of closely related prior works that likely inspired its core idea. LLMs are then prompted to generate a new idea from the set of paper titles and summaries. We introduce a two-axis research-taste taxonomy to profile each idea by its opportunity pattern and research paradigm, and use it to quantify the divergence between human and LLM ideas. Across idea sets generated by different LLMs, we observe a consistent distributional gap: LLM ideas are disproportionately concentrated around bridge-like opportunities and synthesis methods, whereas the human paper reference distribution spreads more broadly across ways of framing gaps and constructing contributions. This result suggests that strong LLMs can produce a range of reasonable ideas, but that range remains narrower than, and systematically shifted relative to, human research taste.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [EmbodimentSemantic: A Spatial Scene-Graph Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Models on Embodied Manipulation Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00020)
*Hassan Jaber,Refinath S N,Luca Cagliero,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmbodimentSemantic，一个用于评估具身操作中空间关系接地能力的数据集和基准，包含真实世界和仿真场景图数据，实验表明现有VLM在深度感知和视角依赖的空间结构理解上存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统虽然能识别物体和遵循语言指令，但缺乏对物体空间关系（如支撑、包含、排序、遮挡、深度关系）的显式表示，这严重限制了机器人在复杂操作任务中的空间接地能力。

Method: 构建了EmbodimentSemantic数据集，以有向物体-关系-物体三元组表示场景图；包含真实世界（SO101机械臂采集）和仿真（基于MuJoCo几何、世界坐标、相机投影和可见性约束自动推导）两部分；并设计了场景图注入VLA策略提示的实验范式来评估下游控制性能。

Result: 开源和商业VLM在合理关系预测上表现尚可，但在精确的深度感知和视角依赖的空间结构理解上存在显著困难。

Conclusion: EmbodimentSemantic为诊断VLM的空间接地能力以及评估其在VLA操作中的实用性提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: Spatial grounding remains a key limitation of vision-language-action (VLA) systems for robotic manipulation. While current models can recognize objects and follow language instructions, they often lack an explicit representation of how objects are arranged in space, including support, containment, ordering, occlusion, and depth-sensitive relations. We introduce EmbodimentSemantic, a spatial scene-graph dataset and benchmark for evaluating relational grounding in embodied manipulation. EmbodimentSemantic represents scenes as directed object-relation-object triplets, where each triplet specifies a spatial relation between an ordered pair of objects using a fixed set of relations. This representation enables direct evaluation of object binding, relation prediction, and spatial consistency. The dataset includes real-world manipulation observations collected with the low-cost SO101 robot arm, together with generated scene graphs for studying spatial grounding in practical robotic settings. To provide controlled validation, we also introduce a simulator-grounded LIBERO benchmark with over 60K manipulation frames and more than 120K camera-specific scene graphs across paired third-person and wrist views, where ground-truth relations are derived automatically from MuJoCo geometry, world coordinates, camera projections, and visibility constraints. We further test whether scene graphs improve downstream control by injecting them into existing VLA policy prompts. Experiments across open-source and commercial VLMs show that current models often predict plausible relations but struggle with exact depth-aware and viewpoint-dependent spatial structure. EmbodimentSemantic provides a unified framework for diagnosing spatial grounding in VLM perception and testing its utility for VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [206] [When to Personalize Household Object Search: A Rigidity-Gated Hybrid Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00022)
*Xianyao Li,Yuhai Wang,Hu Xiao,Kaleb Smith,Gilbert Yang Ye,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PerSim——一种刚性门控混合策略，仅在物体放置行为可变时进行个性化（基于大五人格向量），通过人类校准的仿真管道生成布局多样的物体放置转变，并在统一人类研究中验证：合成转变行为合理（3.85/5分），个性化在低刚性物体上被偏好（p=0.005），离线测试在连续五维特质空间上优于最近离散配置匹配（p=0.035），最终在数字孪生中展示端到端搜索成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人搜索家用物体依赖空间先验，但物体位置随居民特质变化；收集纵向、特质相关的家庭内轨迹具有侵入性且难以扩展，因此需要研究个性化何时有帮助并设计可扩展的方法。

Method: 提出PerSim，一种刚性门控混合策略，结合特质条件先验与人群频率基线，仅在放置行为可变时个性化；构建人类校准的仿真管道生成多样化布局中的物体放置转变，并训练注入连续大五人格向量的预测器，输出房间级先验和房间内共现线索。

Result: 统一人类研究（N=200）验证：(i)合成转变行为合理（均值3.85/5, p<1e-6）；(ii)盲A/B比较中，个性化在低刚性物体上被偏好（p=0.005），人群基线对于普遍放置物体仍然强劲；离线测试中，在连续特质向量上优于最近离散配置匹配（p=0.035）；数字孪生中减少了期望搜索成本。

Conclusion: PerSim通过刚性门控决策规则确定何时个性化，在五维特质空间中实现内插能力，并在端到端搜索成本上展示超越孤立预测指标的改进，为服务机器人个性化搜索提供了可扩展且有效的方法。

Abstract: Service robots searching for household objects rely on spatial priors to reduce search cost, yet object locations can vary with resident traits. Collecting longitudinal, trait-specific in-home trajectories is invasive and hard to scale. We study when personalization helps and propose PerSim, a rigidity-gated hybrid policy that combines a trait-conditioned prior with a population-frequency baseline, personalizing only when placement behavior is variable. To scale resident-conditioned dynamics, we employ a human-calibrated simulation pipeline to generate and validate object-placement transitions in diverse home layouts, and train a predictor that injects continuous Big Five vectors to output room-level priors and within-room co-occurrence cues. In a unified human study (N=200), dual-layer validation shows that (i) synthetic transitions are judged behaviorally plausible (mean 3.85/5, p < 1e-6), and (ii) in a blinded A/B comparison, personalization is favored primarily for low-rigidity objects (p=0.005), while the population-frequency baseline remains strong for universally placed items, yielding a decision rule for when to personalize. In an offline objective test, we observe a small but significant improvement on unseen continuous trait vectors over nearest discrete configuration matching (p=0.035), supporting interpolation in five-dimensional trait space. Finally, in a home digital twin we show that PerSim reduces expected search cost by combining room visitation effort with within-room cue checking, demonstrating end-to-end gains beyond isolated prediction metrics.

</details>


### [207] [Decentralized Geometric Control for Cable-Suspended Payload Transport with Adaptive Mass Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00024)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Paul Schmitt,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GPAC——一种四层分层架构，使多架四旋翼无人机无需中央协调器即可协同运输缆绳悬挂载荷，通过隐式协调（每个无人机独立估计自身载荷份额）和局部缆绳测量实现，仅需低速率邻居位置广播用于避碰，在完整非线性流形上运行，并集成几何控制、防摆、风扰观测器、并行学习质量估计及优先级排序的CBF安全滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同空中运输需要控制器能够处理非线性流形几何、无需集中式协调，并满足运行安全约束，现有方法在这些方面存在不足。

Method: 提出GPAC四层分层架构，核心思想是隐式协调：每架无人机通过局部缆绳测量独立估计有效载荷份额，无需知道无人机数量或载荷质量；仅需低速率邻居位置广播用于避碰；在完整非线性构型流形上运行，集成几何位置与姿态控制、防摆调节、扩展状态观测器、并行学习质量估计及优先级排序的CBF安全滤波器。

Result: 高保真仿真（含柔性缆绳、传感器融合、风湍流）显示平均载荷跟踪RMSE为33.8 cm（13个种子上的变异系数2.8%），每架无人机计算成本低。

Conclusion: GPAC在无中央协调、仅需极少量通信的条件下实现了多四旋翼无人机协同载荷运输，同时满足几何约束和安全要求，具有实用潜力。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial transport requires controllers that respect nonlinear manifold geometry, operate without centralized coordination, and respect operational safety constraints. To address these demands, we present GPAC, a four-layer hierarchical architecture that enables $N$ quadrotors to transport a cable-suspended payload without a central coordinator or by exchanging cable states or adaptive parameters. The key insight is implicit coordination: each quadrotor independently estimates its effective load share from local cable measurements, so combined forces converge to the correct total, even without knowledge of $N$ or the payload mass; the payload position is reconstructed locally from each agent's own cable geometry, and the only inter-agent communication is a low-rate neighbor-position broadcast for collision avoidance. GPAC operates directly on the full nonlinear configuration manifold and integrates geometric position and attitude control, anti-swing regulation, an extended-state observer for wind rejection, concurrent learning-based mass estimation without persistent excitation, and a priority-ordered control barrier function (CBF)-inspired safety filter that reduces operational risk, with input-to-state safety (ISSf) margins that hold exactly under single-constraint activation. A compatibility result shows that the filter's force modifications keep the desired attitude within the almost-global stability region of the $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ attitude controller. Finally, high-fidelity simulation with flexible cables, onboard sensor fusion, and wind turbulence -- with all control and estimation loops closed through the estimator -- yields a mean payload-tracking RMSE of 33.8 cm (2.8\% coefficient of variation over 13 seeds) at a low per-agent computational cost.

</details>


### [208] [FLYNN: Robust Neural Network for Robot Navigation using Fly Brain Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00025)
*Benquan Wang,Jingdao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过果蝇大脑连接组构建神经网络FLYNN，在视觉导航任务中达到与手工网络相当的性能，但在分布外数据和感官缺失条件下表现出显著更强的鲁棒性，即使在完全失明时仍能正常工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在面对新环境或感官剥夺时非常脆弱，而生物系统对此表现出卓越的耐受性，因此希望借鉴生物大脑的连接拓扑来构建更具鲁棒性的人工智能系统。

Method: 直接从果蝇(Drosophila melanogaster)的突触分辨率脑连接组推导出循环神经网络结构，构建FLYNN(果蝇连接组神经网络)，在MuJoCo仿真环境中进行基于视觉的导航任务训练。

Result: FLYNN在导航任务中性能与参数规模相当的现代手工网络持平；在分布外数据和感官丢失场景中鲁棒性显著优于对照网络；即使在完全失明条件下仍能保持功能，而手工网络即使经过特定训练也基本失效；PCA分析表明FLYNN内部状态具有高度的表征模块化。

Conclusion: 该工作证明遵循生物大脑拓扑结构设计人工神经网络是构建抗逆性智能体的一条新方向。

Abstract: While deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art performance in complex tasks, they remain brittle when faced with new environments or sensory deprivation. In contrast, biological systems exhibit remarkable tolerance to these challenges. We address this vulnerability by developing a recurrent neural network (RNN) whose architecture is directly derived from the synaptic-resolution brain connectome of the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster. We demonstrate the feasibility of training the fly connectome neural network (FLYNN) to perform vision-based navigation in MuJoCo, achieving performance comparable to modern hand-crafted networks of similar parameter counts. Crucially, FLYNN exhibits superior resistance to out-of-distribution (OOD) data and tolerance to sensory loss without further training. It remained functional even under total vision loss while hand-crafted networks largely failed, even when specifically trained with camera dropout. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) of the internal state of FLYNN suggests that it exhibits a particularly high degree of representational modularity, which might be related to its robustness. Our work provides a new direction for designing resilient artificial agents following the topology of biological brains.

</details>


### [209] [Invariant Stochastic Filtering on SE(3) for Inertial-Encoder State Estimation of Serial Rigid Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00026)
*S. Yaqubi,J. Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全基于SE(3)李群的串联刚性机械臂不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器(IEKF)，利用运动学方程的群仿射特性实现线性化误差动态自治，通过模块化链式结构使计算复杂度与连杆数成线性关系，并利用李代数李雅普诺夫函数证明了均方指数最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械臂状态估计方法在处理非线性动力学和噪声传播时存在局限性，特别是误差协方差计算的近似化导致精度受限，且计算复杂度随连杆数量增长过快，缺乏可扩展的稳定性保证。

Method: 将IEKF完全建立在SE(3)李群框架下，利用群仿射性质使线性化误差动态自治；提出物理分离的噪声模型，分别处理陀螺仪和加速度计通道；设计模块化链式结构，每个连杆独立运行IEKF，前序连杆通过Adjoint变换传递后验协方差；采用李代数李雅普诺夫函数建立稳定性证明。

Result: 理论结果表明Riccati方程控制真实误差协方差而非局部近似；噪声建模中加速度计通过重力补偿积分提供平移扭转，其测量协方差与采样间隔成比例；科里奥利噪声项在静止时消失并随扭转幅度增长；计算复杂度为O(n)线性于连杆数；通过Adjoint算子范数链式传递实现模块化稳定性证明。

Conclusion: 该方法的群仿射特性确保了精确的误差协方差演化，模块化链式结构实现了线性计算复杂度，李雅普诺夫稳定性分析提供了可扩展至任意链长的稳定性保证，数值结果验证了设计的有效性。

Abstract: An invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) is developed for state estimation of serial rigid manipulators with an arbitrary number of links, formulated entirely within the Lie group SE(3). The group-affine property of the kinematic equations makes the linearised error dynamics autonomous, so the Riccati equation governs the true error covariance rather than a local approximation. A physically separated noise model treats gyroscope and accelerometer channels independently: the accelerometer provides translational twist via gravity-compensated integration, yielding a measurement covariance that scales with the sample interval in exact analogy with process noise discretisation; a state-dependent Coriolis noise term captures gyroscope noise propagating through the nonlinear dynamics, vanishing at rest and growing with twist magnitude. The filter is structured as a modular chain of per-link IEKFs in which the predicted covariance of each link depends on its predecessor only through the Adjoint-transformed posterior, giving linear computational cost in link count. Exponential ultimate boundedness in mean square is established via a Lie algebra Lyapunov function, with per-link bounds chained through the Adjoint operator norm to yield a stability certificate that is modular and scalable to arbitrary chain length. Numerical results validate the design.

</details>


### [210] [Urban Deceleration Behavior Modes Under Scene Context: An Early-Kinematic Classifier from Argoverse 2 Multi-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00027)
*Eni Solomon Laughter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从234段城市驾驶日志中提取1,219个持续减速事件，通过19维运动学特征向量结合K-means聚类与Bootstrap稳定性分析，发现四种稳定减速模式（预期性柔和62.8%、反应性接近30.6%、制动式急动4.8%、异常1.8%），并利用HistGradientBoosting分类器验证了早期窗口急动度是主要预测信号，而车龄是唯一具有中等效应的场景上下文调节变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市减速行为是跟车研究中经验丰富但缺乏系统分类的行为之一，现有研究未能在轨迹层面揭示其内在模式结构，亟需利用感知-equipped自动驾驶数据集进行数据驱动的模式发现与量化分析。

Method: 从Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集234段城市驾驶日志中提取1,219个持续减速事件，编码为19维运动学特征向量；采用K-means聚类（Bootstrap稳定性分析）发现行为模式；使用11个场景上下文变量量化调节作用；构建HistGradientBoosting分类器从事件前1.0秒预测模式归属。

Result: 四种稳定模式：预期性柔和（62.8%）、反应性接近（30.6%）、制动式急动（4.8%）、异常（1.8%），Bootstrap调整兰德指数0.897；仅车龄具有中等效应（ε²=0.085）；早期分类器在1.0秒时宏F1=0.758，场景上下文贡献+0.059 F1；模式在中速行驶时具有不变性（ARI=0.817），低速时依赖驾驶体制（ARI=0.166）。

Conclusion: 少量稳定的运动学模式即可结构化城市减速行为；早期时间窗口内的急动度是主要预测信号；车龄（pair age）是首要的场景上下文调节变量，而场景几何和弱势道路使用者接近度影响可忽略。

Abstract: Urban deceleration is one of the most empirically studied yet least taxonomically organized behaviors in car-following research. Recent perception-equipped autonomous-vehicle datasets enable trajectory-anchored mode discovery. We extract 1,219 sustained deceleration events from 234 urban driving logs of the Argoverse 2 Sensor dataset, encode each event in a 19-dimensional kinematic feature vector, discover behavioral modes via K-means clustering with bootstrap stability analysis, and quantify modulation by eleven scene-context variables. A HistGradientBoosting classifier predicts mode membership from the first 1.0 s of each event. Four stable modes emerge with a bootstrap Adjusted Rand Index of 0.897 across 50 resamples: anticipatory soft (62.8%), reactive closing (30.6%), brake-like jerk (4.8%), and an outlier category (1.8%). Only pair age shows a medium effect (epsilon^2 = 0.085); scene geometry and vulnerable-road-user proximity show negligible effects. The early-event classifier achieves macro-F1 = 0.758 at 1.0 s, with scene context contributing +0.059 F1 over kinematics alone. Modes are regime-invariant in medium-speed driving (ARI = 0.817) but regime-dependent at low speed (ARI = 0.166). A small set of stable kinematic modes structures urban deceleration; early-window jerk dominates predictive signal; and pair age is the primary contextual modulator.

</details>


### [211] [Trajectory Learning with Graph Representations for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00028)
*Berke Kartal,Burcu Kilic,Yigit Yildirim,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图注意力网络和轨迹级学习目标的模仿学习框架，通过编码时空社交动态实现移动机器人的社会合规导航，在仿真和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在实现社会合规导航时存在不足：强化学习依赖手工设计的奖励函数，难以复现真实行人行为模式；模仿学习缺乏社交交互建模且存在误差累积问题。两者均未能同时处理真实数据中的空间和时间特征。

Method: 提出模仿学习框架：1) 引入基于图的辅助网络，通过注意力机制编码人群交互状态以表示社交上下文；2) 设计导航模块，通过编码状态预测和轨迹级学习目标捕获时间动态并缓解误差累积。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界数据集上，该框架在多种社会指标上优于已建立的数据驱动基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合图注意力网络和轨迹级学习目标的模仿学习框架能有效建模时空社交动态，提升移动机器人导航的社会合规性。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots are expected to exhibit socially compliant navigation for minimizing pedestrian disturbance. While capturing social interactions and incorporating pedestrian motion estimations into decision-making are beneficial for compliance, prior methods fail to address both spatial and temporal characteristics present in real-world data. Reinforcement Learning offers high capability, but it requires hand-crafted reward functions that reduce social behavior to static criteria, limiting its ability to reproduce patterns that exist in real pedestrian behavior. Imitation Learning offers direct training from real-world data but lacks modeling of social interactions and suffers from error accumulation. To this end, we propose an imitation learning framework that leverages spatiotemporal dynamics for socially compliant navigation. To represent social context based on interactions, we introduce a graph-based auxiliary network that encodes crowd states by attending to pedestrians. In addition, we present a navigation module that captures temporal dynamics and mitigates error accumulations by incorporating encoded state predictions and employing a trajectory-level learning objective. Our framework outperforms established data-driven baselines on simulation and a real-world dataset across diverse social metrics.

</details>


### [212] [Memory-Native Non-Terrestrial Networks for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00029)
*Chengyang Li,Yikun Wang,Jiahui He,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Yuan Wu,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MemNTN范式，通过双记忆架构（物理记忆+数字记忆）实现记忆原生的非地面网络优化，在卫星具身问答任务中显著优于传统无状态NTN和地面方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非地面网络协议是无记忆的，决策仅由局部信道条件和瞬时服务需求驱动，在高动态、资源受限、拓扑变化的任务导向环境中效率低下，无法满足具身智能体与云端协同的需求。

Method: 建立双记忆架构，区分代表世界状态的物理记忆和编码历史网络经验的数字记忆；设计记忆的获取、压缩、估值、更新和利用机制，实现跨层（物理层到应用层）的记忆原生决策。

Result: 在卫星具身问答任务中，MemNTN显著优于传统无状态NTN和地面方法。

Conclusion: 记忆原生的NTN范式能够有效利用长跨度上下文信息，解决高动态网络环境下的跨层优化问题，为具身智能与非地面网络的深度融合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) provide ubiquitous connectivity for embodied intelligence (EI), enabling robots in wilderness to leverage cloud resources or report critical information to remote centers. However, the synergy is nontrivial due to the highly-dynamic, resource-constrained, topology-varying, and task-oriented environment. Existing memoryless NTN protocols become inefficient, since the decisions are driven by local channel conditions and instantaneous service demands. To address these limitations, this paper proposes the memory-native NTN (MemNTN) paradigm that leverages long-horizon contexts for memory augmented system optimization. To realize this paradigm shift, we establish a dual-memory architecture that distinguishes between physical memory representing the state of the world and digital memory encoding historical network experience. We develop memory acquisition, compression, valuation, update, and utilization mechanisms that facilitate cross-layer, memory-native decision-making, spanning from the physical and access layers up to the network and application layers. Experiments in satellite embodied question answering (SEQA) demonstrate that the proposed MemNTN significantly outperforms conventional stateless NTN and terrestrial approaches.

</details>


### [213] [A Unified Benchmark for RCM-Constrained Visual Servoing: Modeling-Controller Interaction and Robustness Analysis in Laparoscopic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00030)
*Jing Zhang,Mengtang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的开源仿真框架，集成了三种代表性RCM建模方法和六种IBVS控制架构，用于系统评估机器人辅助腹腔镜微创手术中的RCM约束视觉伺服控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于控制的RCM策略虽被广泛采用，但由于缺乏统一且可复现的基准，不同RCM公式和IBVS框架之间的系统比较仍然困难。

Method: 在统一的速度级公式下，集成了三种代表性RCM建模方法和六种IBVS控制架构，构建了可控且一致的评估框架。

Result: 通过结构化案例研究，揭示了建模与控制交互中的关键结构敏感性，包括切平面定义、约束维度、开环与闭环执行以及运动学奇点附近的鲁棒性影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为RCM约束视觉伺服研究提供了可复现的基础，相关资源已开源。

Abstract: In robot-assisted laparoscopic minimally invasive surgery (MIS), accurate enforcement of the remote center of motion (RCM) constraint is critical for safe and stable automatic field-of-view (FoV) adjustment. Although control-based RCM strategies are widely adopted due to their flexibility and cost-effectiveness, systematic comparison of different RCM formulations and image-based visual servoing (IBVS) frameworks remains challenging due to the lack of a unified and reproducible benchmark. This paper presents an open-source simulation framework integrating three representative RCM modeling approaches and six IBVS-based control architectures within a unified velocity-level formulation, enabling controlled and consistent evaluation. Through structured case studies, the framework reveals key structural sensitivities arising from modeling and controller interactions, including the impact of tangent-plane definition, constraint dimensionality, open- versus closed-loop enforcement, and robustness near kinematic singularities. All resources are released and demostrations are provided in the supplementary video, providing a reproducible foundation for RCM-constrained visual servoing research.

</details>


### [214] [Joint Discovery of Object and Action Symbols through Effect Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00031)
*Burcu Kilic,Berke Kartal,Fatih Dogangun,Erhan Oztop,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过二值瓶颈层联合发现操作原语和物体类别的模型，利用中间步骤实现部分动作执行，并在行为相似性基础上实现小样本泛化，在桌面重定位和堆叠任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作要么仅基于视觉外观分类物体（无法区分外观相似但行为不同的物体），要么基于交互效果分类但受限于预定义动作，缺乏同时学习操作原语和物体类别的能力。

Method: 通过二值瓶颈层联合发现高层操作原语和物体类别，预测多模态交互结果（物体运动、接触、力反馈）；利用预测效果轨迹的中间步骤实现部分动作执行；通过少量交互效果与学习到的物体符号预测效果对比实现基于行为的小样本泛化。

Result: 在桌面重定位和堆叠任务中，效果驱动的规划方法在规划精度上优于最先进方法和基于视觉的替代方法，且对已知和新物体均有效。

Conclusion: 基于交互效果而非视觉外观的抽象表示，结合离散规划与部分动作执行，能够有效提升操作规划的精度与泛化能力。

Abstract: To perform complex manipulation planning, autonomous robots are required to abstract continuous, high-dimensional sensorimotor interactions into discrete object and action representations. Earlier work either categorized objects based on visual appearances, which fails to distinguish objects that appear similar but behave differently, or based on effects under interaction, but was limited to predefined actions. To address these limitations, we propose a model that jointly discovers high-level manipulation primitives and object categories through a binary bottleneck layer, trained to predict multi-modal outcomes, including object motion, contact, and force feedback, from random interaction data. Building on these discovered binary representations, we leverage a discrete planning method that uses intermediate steps in the predicted effect trajectory to enable partial action executions for precise low-level control. Additionally, we evaluate our framework's generalization capabilities on novel objects by assigning object categories through comparing a small number of interaction effects with the predicted effects of learned object symbols, enabling few-shot generalization based on behavior rather than visual similarity. We conduct experiments on tabletop repositioning and stacking tasks, and confirm that our effect-driven planning approach outperforms both a state-of-the-art method and a visual-based alternative in planning precision across seen and novel objects.

</details>


### [215] [Learning Dexterous Manipulation Using Contact Wrench Guidance From Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00033)
*Xinghao Zhu,Zixi Liu,Shalin Jain,Chenran Li,Milad Noori,Huihua Zhao,John Welsh,Michael Andres Lin,Wei Liu,Tingwu Wang,Xingye Da,Zhengyi Luo,Vishal Kulkarni,Naema Bhatti,Yuke Zhu,Linxi Fan,Bowen Wen,Danfei Xu,Soha Pouya,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHORD是一种利用人手演示的接触力/力矩空间引导来训练机器人灵巧操作策略的框架，在大规模仿真基准上取得82.12%平均成功率，并成功迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据丰富但难以直接迁移到机器人策略，现有方法在处理接触丰富的灵巧操作任务时扩展性不足。

Method: 提出以物体为中心的接触力/力矩空间引导（Contact Wrench Space Guidance），将人与机器人的运动统一表示为可施加在物体上的力与力矩，通过诱导的瞬时运动来度量相似性，以此引导强化学习训练。

Result: 在4,739个双手灵巧操作任务的仿真基准（其中评估1,831个任务）上达到82.12%平均成功率；从仅手部演示和第三人称视角演示泛化到全身操作任务时成功率达90.77%；学到的策略在真实世界的开环和闭环设置中均成功迁移。

Conclusion: 接触力/力矩空间引导有效提升了强化学习在接触丰富灵巧操作中的可扩展性，且跨设置泛化能力与真实世界迁移能力俱佳。

Abstract: Dexterous robot manipulation can benefit from the abundance of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to robot policies remains challenging. We present Contact Wrench Guidance from Human Demonstration in Robotic Dexterous Manipulation (CHORD), a framework for long-horizon manipulation of rigid and articulated objects with reinforcement learning. The key idea is object-centric contact wrench space guidance: we represent human and robot motions by the forces and torques they can induce on the object, enabling similarity to be measured by the induced instantaneous motions. This guidance makes reinforcement learning more scalable for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. We further introduce a large-scale simulation benchmark with 4,739 bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks, constructed from motion-capture datasets and reconstructed in-house videos. Evaluated on 1,831 benchmark tasks, CHORD achieves an average success rate of 82.12%, demonstrating strong scalability. CHORD also generalizes to whole-body manipulation from hand-only and third-person demonstrations, achieving a 90.77% success rate, and the learned policies transfer to the real world in both open-loop and closed-loop settings.

</details>


### [216] [Optimal any-angle path planning in static and dynamic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00065)
*Yiyuan Zou,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Zeta*和Zeta*-SIPP两种最优任意角度路径规划算法，通过椭圆前向扩展和视场两种通用技术加速计算，在静态和动态环境下均保持最优性。Zeta*-SIPP比现有最优规划器快20倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意角度路径规划算法中，只有少数能保证最优解，尤其在动态障碍物存在时。为克服这一挑战，需设计既能保证最优性又能高效计算的通用方法。

Method: 提出两种通用加速技术：1) 椭圆前向扩展，利用椭圆邻域限制搜索空间；2) 视场技术，替代传统视线方法加速可见性检查。引入反向扫描和前向扫描来整合这两种技术，并在此基础上构建Zeta*（静态环境）和Zeta*-SIPP（动态环境）。

Result: Zeta*结合前向扫描时与最优算法Anya性能相当，且更易扩展到动态场景。Zeta*-SIPP无论采用哪种扫描方式，速度均比当前最优规划器TO-AA-SIPP快20倍以上。

Conclusion: 该研究识别了实现最优任意角度路径规划的关键要素，并提出了一套适用于不同环境的统一方法。

Abstract: Any-angle path planning extends traditional graph-based path planning by allowing movement between any pair of vertices, rather than being restricted by predefined edges. It can find straighter and shorter paths in continuous space with graphs, making it particularly suitable for navigation in open areas such as airspaces, warehouses, and oceans. Many any-angle path-planning algorithms have been proposed, but only a few can guarantee optimal solutions, especially in the presence of dynamic obstacles. To address this challenge, this article focuses on optimal any-angle path planning on grids and introduces two general techniques that accelerate computation while preserving optimality in both static and dynamic environments: 1) elliptical forward expansion, which leverages ellipse-based neighborhoods to restrict the search space, and 2) field of view, which replaces traditional line-of-sight methods to speed up visibility checks. To integrate these two techniques, inverted and forward scanning are introduced. Inverted scanning establishes visual connections from open nodes, whereas forward scanning initiates scans from closed nodes. Building on the proposed techniques, Zeta* and Zeta*-SIPP are developed for static and dynamic environments respectively. Zeta*, when combined with forward scanning, is similar to the state-of-the-art algorithm Anya and attains comparable performance. Unlike Anya, Zeta* can be readily extended to other settings, such as dynamic environments (e.g., Zeta*-SIPP). Zeta*-SIPP, with either scanning method, is more than 20 times faster than the corresponding state-of-the-art optimal planner TO-AA-SIPP. Overall, this research identifies the key requirements for achieving optimal any-angle path planning and introduces a unified approach suitable for different environments.

</details>


### [217] [Learning Expert Strategy for Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Intervention via Decoupled Procedural Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00066)
*Yanxi Chen,Tianliang Yao,Shaolong Tang,Jiyuan Zhao,Hengyu Hu,Zhaoxing Li,Antonio J. Sánchez Egea,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将强化学习高层策略与专家执行模块解耦的自主血管内介入导航框架，在仿真和真实机器人平台上实现了>96%导航成功率、29.3%操作步骤减少和13%轨迹方差降低，显著提升了手术标准化程度与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航有望标准化手术质量并降低手动操作固有的性能变异性，但现有强化学习方法难以满足明确的约束满足和安全保障需求。

Method: 将高层战略决策与低层执行显式解耦：战略RL策略生成全局导航意图，专家知悉执行模块确保机器人运动严格遵循专家操作规范、实时运动学限制和血管安全约束。

Result: 高保真3D仿真与真实机器人平台实验表明：导航成功率>96%，操作步骤减少29.3%，轨迹方差降低13%，性能优于基线策略并能复现专家级水平。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解耦策略-执行架构提升了机器人血管内介入的可预测性、安全性和一致性，具有标准化临床操作的潜力。

Abstract: Endovascular interventions are high-stakes procedures requiring precise device operation within complex and tortuous vascular anatomies. Autonomous endovascular navigation has the potential to standardize procedural quality and reduce the performance variability inherent in manual operation. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches have demonstrated promise in enabling autonomy in endovascular intervention, they often struggle with explicit constraint satisfaction and safety guarantees. To address these challenges, a learning-based expert strategy is introduced, enhancing procedural consistency in autonomous endovascular intervention by explicitly decoupling high-level strategic decision-making from low-level procedural execution. The proposed framework replicates the expert clinical decision-making process: a strategic RL policy generates global navigation intents, which are subsequently refined through an expert-informed execution module. This module ensures that robot movements strictly adhere to expert operational norms, real-time kinematic limits, and vessel safety constraints. Experimental evaluation across high-fidelity 3D simulations and a real-world robotic platform demonstrates that the proposed framework not only outperforms baseline policies but also effectively replicates expert-level proficiency. The framework achieves a high navigation success rate (> 96%) and a 29.3% reduction in operational steps, which translates to enhanced operative efficiency and minimized device-vessel interaction. Furthermore, a 13% reduction in trajectory variance indicates superior procedural standardization, aligning autonomous behavior with established clinical norms. These results underscore its potential to enhance the predictability, safety, and consistency of robotic endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [218] [AD-MPCC: Adaptive Differentiable Model Predictive Contouring Control for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00141)
*Nam T. Nguyen,Binh Nguyen,Ahmad Amine,Thanh Vo-Duy,Rahul Mangharam,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应可微模型预测轮廓控制（AD-MPCC）框架，通过集成可微MPCC与在线参数估计，实现在变化路面条件下的自动驾驶赛车控制。利用参数化Pacejka魔术公式和正则化移动时域估计方案在线更新参数，并通过Diff-MPCC框架基于长期性能成本优化目标权重，同时采用Pacejka-informed机器学习模型实现目标权重的在线自适应。仿真表明AD-MPCC在单表面和多表面场景下均能保证安全并实现更快的圈速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶赛车控制方法难以应对变化的道路表面条件（如不同摩擦系数路面），传统MPCC缺乏对时变环境参数的在线估计机制，且目标权重通常需要手工调参，无法根据长期性能目标自动优化。

Method: 1）采用参数化Pacejka魔术公式结合正则化移动时域估计（带有指数衰减权重）在线估计轮胎-道路交互参数；2）提出可微MPCC（Diff-MPCC）框架，通过可微优化实现基于长期性能成本的目标权重最优调整；3）训练Pacejka-informed机器学习模型，通过Diff-MPCC生成的监督数据学习目标权重的在线调优。

Result: 在单表面和多表面场景的仿真中，AD-MPCC相比基线控制器能可靠地保证安全性并实现更快的圈速。

Conclusion: 将可微MPCC与在线参数估计相结合，通过数据驱动的方式自适应优化目标权重，是应对变化路面条件、提升自动驾驶赛车性能的有效框架。

Abstract: This paper presents Adaptive Differentiable Model Predictive Contouring Control (AD-MPCC), a framework for autonomous racing that integrates differentiable MPCC with online parameter estimation to handle varying road-surface conditions. For online parameter estimation, we leverage a parameterized Pacejka Magic Formula together with a regularized moving-horizon estimation scheme with exponentially decaying weights to capture road interactions and update parameters in real time. Furthermore, we propose a differentiable MPCC (Diff-MPCC) framework that enables optimal adjustment of objective weights based on predefined long-horizon performance costs. To implement Diff-MPCC for online objective weight adaptation, we propose a Pacejka-informed machine learning model that is trained in a supervised manner using data generated by Diff-MPCC to tune the objective weights. Simulation results demonstrate that AD-MPCC reliably ensures safety and achieves faster lap times compared to baseline controllers in both single-surface and multiple-surface scenarios.

</details>


### [219] [Stop Pretending Social Robots Are Inevitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00142)
*Serge Thill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文批评RO-MAN和HRI会议将人机共融社会描绘为不可避免的未来，认为这种假设性框架扭曲了研究方向：优先设计想象性场景而非提升机器人实际能力，忽视真实社会需求，削弱技术发展。主张拒绝"必然的社会机器人"叙事，转而关注支撑人机交互中"智能体(I)"的认知机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RO-MAN和HRI会议将人机共融社会描绘为不可避免趋势的倾向，指出这种表述对研究方向的塑造作用，呼吁反思并修正领域的研究框架。

Method: 通过批判性分析，指出将人机共融社会视为既定事实所带来的三个问题：1)使用户研究脱离生态效度；2)使不假设机器人是解决方案的真实社会需求研究被边缘化；3)削弱真正使机器人成为社会智能体而非任务自动化工具的技术开发。

Result: 指出假设性框架不仅助长应抵制的技术解决主义，损害科学进步，而且适得其反，会扼杀实现该未来所需的研究。

Conclusion: 应拒绝以"不可避免的社会机器人"来框架化和推广该领域，转而采纳能促进领域进步（无论未来如何）的研究框架；建议重新聚焦于人机交互中"智能体(I)"所需的认知机制。

Abstract: This paper takes issue with the recent themes of both the RO-MAN and the HRI conferences for their portrayal of a future human-robot society as inevitable. The focus is on discussing how such statements ultimately shape research. By treating a future human-robot society as a fait accompli, license is given for user studies to imagine any scenario they like, no matter whether it has any ecological relevance, and to emphasise the scenario design over actually creating robot abilities needed to fullfill the imagined role. Meanwhile, research that focusses on actual societal needs, without assuming that robots are a solution, is deprioritised, as is technical development, in particular with respect to abilities that are necessary to enable robots that function as social agents rather than a mere automation of tasks. A frame that simply assumes a robot future not only detracts from scientific advancement in favour of a techno-solutionism we ought to resist, it is also self-defeating as it risks stifling the research needed to bring it about. We should therefore reject attempts to frame and promote the field in terms of the inevitable social robot and instead focus on one that facilitates advances in the field regardless of what the future holds. This paper suggests that a renewed focus on cognitive mechanisms necessary for the "I" in HRI would be a good starting point.

</details>


### [220] [Iterated Invariant EKF for 3D Landmark-Aided Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00145)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF）应用于基于地标的惯性三维定位，通过数值仿真证明其在估计精度和一致性上优于传统SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SO(3)-EKF存在虚假可观测性问题，导致滤波器在不可观测方向上过度自信，估计性能下降；IEKF虽改进了系统建模但测量更新不完全满足状态兼容性；需要更优的滤波方法用于地标辅助惯性导航。

Method: 将IterIEKF公式化并应用于基于地标的惯性三维定位问题，利用李群上的群仿射系统特性，在低噪声条件下保证估计状态处于观测流形上，同时将不确定性约束在切空间内。

Result: 通过数值仿真，IterIEKF在估计精度和一致性两方面均优于SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF。

Conclusion: IterIEKF有效解决了地标辅助惯性导航中的虚假可观测性问题，是提升三维定位性能的有效方法。

Abstract: Inertial navigation systems aided by three-dimensional landmark measurements constitute a fundamental problem in robotic perception and state estimation. Classical SO(3)-based Extended Kalman Filter (SO(3)-EKF) approaches provide practical solutions, but suffer from the false observability problem, in which the filter becomes overconfident in unobservable directions, leading to degraded estimation performance. The Invariant EKF (IEKF) addresses this limitation by reformulating the system dynamics as a group-affine system on a Lie group, although its measurement update does not fully satisfy certain state compatibility properties. More recently, the Iterated Invariant EKF (IterIEKF) was proposed to further improve the IEKF by ensuring, in the low-noise regime, that the estimated state remains on the observed state manifold while the uncertainty is confined to its tangent space. In this work, we formulate and apply the IterIEKF to landmark-based inertial 3D localization for the first time. Through numerical simulations, we show that the proposed approach outperforms the classical SO(3)-EKF, the Iterated SO(3)-EKF, and the IEKF in terms of both estimation accuracy and consistency.

</details>


### [221] [3D Point World Models: Point Completion Enables More Accurate Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00148)
*Skand Peri,Hung Nguyen,Chanho Kim,Li Fuxin,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出3D点云世界模型(3DPWM)，通过先完成部分点云再学习动作条件动力学，实现了在完整3D空间中操作的任务无关世界模型，支持长程规划（100-300+步）并成功实现仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的动力学模型缺乏显式3D空间结构，存在几何不一致的长程 rollout 误差累积问题；而基于部分点云的3D动力学模型虽改善了几何一致性，但仍对遮挡和累积预测漂移敏感。

Method: 3DPWM首先对部分点云进行补全以获得完整3D场景几何，然后在该完整场景上学习动作条件动力学模型。模型完全在3D空间运行，支持任务无关的模型预测控制规划。

Result: 在多种机器人形态和桌面操控基准上，3DPWM实现了显著更可靠的长程 rollout（100-300+步），支持开环和闭环规划，并成功实现了仿真到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: 通过在完整3D几何上学习动力学，3DPWM有效解决了遮挡和累积漂移问题，为实现可靠的长期模型预测控制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of the world enables robotic control through planning, potentially allowing robots to improvise solutions on new tasks. However, large video-based dynamics models lack explicit 3D spatial structure and suffer from geometrically inconsistent long-term rollouts with compounding errors. Emerging 3D dynamics models based on partial point clouds improve geometric consistency but remain sensitive to occlusions and accumulated prediction drift. To address these challenges, we present 3D Point World Models (3DPWM) - a task-agnostic world model that operates entirely in 3D space by first completing partial point clouds and then learning action-conditioned dynamics in this completed 3D scene. By operating on completed geometry, 3DPWM enables reliable long-horizon rollouts and more accurate cost evaluation for model-based planning while supporting adaptation to new tasks. Experiments across different robotic embodiments and tabletop manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that 3DPWM achieves significantly more reliable long-horizon rollouts (100-300+ steps), supports both open-loop and closed-loop planning, and enables successful sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [222] [Dual-Informed Vertical Expansion for Multi-Objective Node Selection in Anytime Conflict-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00156)
*Willem van Osselaer,Jiarui Li,Meshal Alharbi,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Informed Vertical Expansion (DIVE)，一种用于精确CBS算法的高级节点选择策略，通过结合最佳边界搜索与深度优先搜索的优势，在保持最优性的同时减少搜索前沿大小、提供早期可行解，并实现更高效的内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CBS算法中最佳优先节点选择虽能最小化展开节点数，但会导致前沿队列庞大、打断父子扩展序列，且无法在终止前提供可行解。作者将节点选择视为CBS的一等设计选择，旨在改进这些缺陷。

Method: 提出DIVE策略：每次dive从当前最佳边界节点开始，在dive内采用深度优先方向探索有前景的子节点，利用父子局部性，并通过剪枝限制无效探索。从分支定界视角形式化CBS节点选择，证明改变遍历策略不影响最优性保证。

Result: 实验表明DIVE一致地减少了dive中断次数，提供具有认证间隙的早期可行解，相比最佳优先搜索大幅降低队列内存使用，且在密集或内存受限场景中受益于热启动和简单响应变体。

Conclusion: 三种策略各有互补优势：最佳优先搜索节点效率高，迭代加深内存效率高，DIVE在保持最佳边界重锚的基础上实现了dive效率高。DIVE为CBS节点选择提供了新的权衡维度。

Abstract: Conflict-Based Search (CBS) is a leading exact algorithm for Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF), but its high-level node-selection rule is usually treated as a fixed implementation detail. Standard best-first selection is strong for minimizing expanded nodes and closing the optimality certificate, yet it can maintain a large frontier, interrupt parent-child expansion sequences, and provide no feasible incumbent until termination. This paper studies node selection as a first-class design choice for exact CBS. We introduce Dual-Informed Vertical Expansion (DIVE), a policy that is best-bound between dives and depth-oriented within a dive. DIVE starts each dive from the current best-bound frontier, follows promising children to exploit parent-child locality, and uses incumbent pruning to limit unproductive excursions. We formalize CBS node selection through a branch-and-bound view, prove that the traversal policy can be changed without affecting exactness, and analyze the resulting trade-offs among expanded nodes, dive breaks, queue size, and primal-dual bound progress. The analysis predicts three complementary extremes. Best-first search is node efficient, iterative deepening is memory efficient, and DIVE is dive efficient while retaining regular best-bound reanchoring. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks support this trade-off map. DIVE consistently reduces dive breaks, provides early incumbents with certified gaps, uses substantially less queue memory than best-first search, and benefits from warm starts and simple responsive variants in dense or memory-limited regimes.

</details>


### [223] [Distributed Multi Robot Lunar Cargo Transportation via Phase Decomposed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00160)
*Ashutosh Mishra,Elian Neppel,Shreya Santra,Antoine Jonquières,Muhammad Athallah Naufal,Kentaro Uno,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种相位分解强化学习框架，用于模块化可重构机器人在月球任务中的协同货物运输，通过将任务分解为抬升、运输和放置三个阶段，结合集中训练与分布式部署，在仿真和JAXA实验设施中验证了各阶段的可靠运输能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化可重构机器人系统为未来月球任务中的协同表面操作提供了可扩展方案，但协同货物运输面临形态依赖的拓扑变化、强载荷耦合、长时序决策及安全约束等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出相位分解强化学习框架：(1) 将任务分解为抬升、运输、放置三个阶段，每个阶段使用专用联合状态策略捕获智能体间耦合；(2) 集中训练保证收敛稳定性，部署时仅用本体感知进行控制；(3) 确定性相位控制器（马尔可夫状态表示）调控阶段转换；(4) 故障敏感同步机制确保协调推进和安全感知的实时停止。

Result: 在仿真和JAXA空间探索实验设施的受控现场实验中，该框架在所有阶段均实现了可靠的协同运输。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够有效解决模块化可重构机器人协同货物运输中的拓扑变化、载荷耦合、长时序决策和安全约束等核心挑战，在仿真与实物实验中均表现出色，为月球任务中的协同操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modular reconfigurable robotic systems provide a scalable solution for cooperative surface operations in future lunar missions. However, cooperative cargo transportation remains challenging due to morphology-dependent topology changes, strong payload-induced coupling, long-horizon decision making, and safety constraints. This paper proposes a phase-decomposed reinforcement learning framework for cooperative cargo transport with distributed robotic units. The task is decomposed into lifting, transportation, and placement, each optimized with a dedicated joint-state policy capturing inter-agent coupling. Centralized training promotes stable convergence, while deployment uses onboard proprioception for control and OptiTrack motion capture for ground-truth evaluation and post-processed metrics. A deterministic phase controller expressed in Markov state representation regulates transitions between stages, and a failure-sensitive synchronization mechanism ensures coordinated progression and safety-aware halting during real-world execution. The framework is evaluated in simulation and through controlled field experiments at a JAXA space exploration test facility. Results demonstrate reliable cooperative transport across all stages in both simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [224] [HydraCollab: Adaptive Collaborative-Perception for Distributed Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00191)
*Luke Chen,Cheng-Ju Wu,David R. Martin,Qilin Ye,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HydraCollab 提出了一种自适应协同感知框架，通过选择性传输最有效的传感器特征并结合空间置信度图动态切换协作策略（中间层或后层融合），在降低通信带宽的同时保持甚至提升感知精度，在多个数据集上相较 SOTA 方法 Where2comm 实现了带宽降低 59%-74% 且性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知系统面临通信带宽与感知精度之间的固有权衡，高带宽传输带来更好的感知结果但增加了通信开销，而真实世界的网络存在带宽约束，亟需在不牺牲感知性能的前提下最小化通信开销。

Method: HydraCollab 框架包含两个核心设计：(i) 选择性传输最具信息量的传感器特征；(ii) 基于空间置信度图动态自适应地选择协作策略（中间层融合或后层融合）。

Result: 在 V2X-R、V2X-Radar 和 UAV3D-mini 数据集上，HydraCollab 实现了精度与通信成本之间的最佳整体平衡。相比 Where2comm，在 V2X-R 上仅使用 41% 的带宽，在 V2X-Radar 上仅使用 26% 的带宽，同时分别提升性能 0.78% 和 0.75%。

Conclusion: HydraCollab 是一种有效的自适应协同感知框架，能够在带宽受限的真实通信网络下实现精度与通信开销的最优权衡。

Abstract: Collaborative-perception enables multi-robot systems to enhance situational awareness by sharing perceptual information. Existing collaborative-perception systems face an inherent trade-off between communication bandwidth requirements and perception accuracy, where methods that exchange more information achieve better perception results at the cost of increased communication overhead. However, real-world communication networks impose bandwidth constraints that require minimizing communication overhead without sacrificing perception performance. To address this challenge, we propose HydraCollab, an adaptive collaborative-perception framework that (i) selectively transmits the most informative sensor features and (ii) dynamically employs collaboration strategies (intermediate or late) based on spatial confidence maps. Extensive evaluations on the V2X-R, V2X-Radar and UAV3D-mini datasets demonstrate that HydraCollab achieves the best overall trade-off between accuracy and communication cost among existing collaborative-perception methods. Relative to SOTA Where2comm, HydraCollab uses only 41% of the bandwidth on V2X-R and 26% on V2X-Radar while improving performance by 0.78% and 0.75% respectively. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AICPS/HydraCollab.

</details>


### [225] [ELMP: Efficient Learning for Motion Planning via Analytical Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00215)
*Yixiao Li,Tifanny Portela,Jordis Herrmann,René Zurbrügg,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELMP通过自监督微调（无需专家数据）实现神经运动规划器的高效适应，使用可微运动学层和点云编码工具几何，在新环境中成功率从57.3%提升至89.8%，适应成本降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经运动规划器（NMPs）能实现快速反应式运动生成，但适应新环境需要重新收集大量专家数据集，计算成本过高。

Method: 提出ELMP框架：1）自监督微调，通过可微运动学层直接优化策略，使用密集碰撞、目标到达和平滑性目标函数；2）用点云显式编码工具几何信息，支持不同运动链的泛化。

Result: ELMP平均成功率84.8%，冷启动延迟比经典方法低数个数量级；在未见环境中，自监督微调将成功率从57.3%（零样本）提升至89.8%；推理延迟保持在毫秒级；在Franka Emika Panda机器人上验证。

Conclusion: ELMP通过无需专家数据的自监督微调，显著降低了神经运动规划器适应新环境的成本，同时保持了高性能和实时推理能力，消除了数据收集瓶颈。

Abstract: Neural Motion Planners (NMPs) enable fast reactive motion generation, but adapting them to new environments typically requires recollecting large expert datasets, which is computationally prohibitive. We propose ELMP, a framework for data-efficient adaptation via self-supervised fine-tuning. Rather than generating additional expert trajectories with expensive global planners, ELMP directly optimizes the policy through a differentiable kinematic layer using dense collision, target-reaching, and smoothness objectives. This replaces expert data generation with rapid problem sampling, reducing per-sample adaptation cost by roughly two orders of magnitude. To further support robust generalization across changing kinematic chains, we introduce a mechanism to explicitly encode tool geometry via point clouds. Benchmarked against classical and neural baselines, ELMP achieves an 84.8% average success rate with orders-of-magnitude lower cold-start latency than classical methods. In unseen environments, self-supervised fine-tuning improves success rate from 57.3% (zero-shot) to 89.8%, removing the data collection bottleneck. Our approach maintains millisecond-level inference latency and is validated on a physical Franka Emika Panda robot.

</details>


### [226] [ASPIRE: Agentic /Skills Discovery for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00272)
*Runyu Lu,Yubo Wu,Ethan Kou,Letian Fu,Wenli Xiao,Ajay Mandlekar,Yinzhen Xu,Guanya Shi,Ken Goldberg,Ang Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Guanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASPIRE是一个持续学习系统，通过代码即策略范式自主编写和优化机器人控制程序，并不断积累可复用的技能库，在多项基准测试中显著超越现有方法，并支持零样本泛化与仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要协调多模态感知、管理物理接触动力学、处理多样化配置和执行失败，过程极具挑战性，因此需要一种能够自主编程并持续学习的系统来降低编程门槛和提升鲁棒性。

Method: ASPIRE包含三个核心组件：(1) 闭环机器人执行引擎，提供细粒度多模态轨迹以支持自主故障诊断、修复合成与验证；(2) 持续扩展的技能库，将验证后的修复方案蒸馏为可复用、可迁移的知识；(3) 进化搜索机制，生成多样化的任务序列和控制程序以超越单轨迹的优化范围。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro扰动测试中优于先前方法最高77%，在Robosuite双手交接任务中提升72%，在BEHAVIOR-1K长时域家务任务中提升32%。在LIBERO-Pro Long零样本长时域任务中，ASPIRE成功率31% vs 对比方法4%。仿真习得的技能可有效迁移到真实机器人，显著减少编程工作量。

Conclusion: ASPIRE通过闭环执行、技能库积累和进化搜索三者协同，实现了机器人控制程序的自主编写和持续改进，在多个复杂任务中表现优异，并初步验证了仿真到现实的技能迁移能力，为机器人自主编程提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Traditional robot programming is challenging: it requires orchestrating multimodal perception, managing physical contact dynamics, and handling diverse configurations and execution failures. We introduce ASPIRE (Agentic Skill Programming through Iterative Robot Exploration), a continual learning system that autonomously writes and refines robot control programs in a code-as-policy paradigm while compounding experience into a reusable skill library. ASPIRE discovers skills that persist across tasks, simulation and real-world settings, and embodiments. It operates in an open-ended loop with three components: (1) a closed-loop robot execution engine that exposes fine-grained multimodal traces, enabling autonomous failure diagnosis, repair synthesis, and validation; (2) a continually expanding skill library that distills validated fixes into reusable, transferable knowledge; and (3) evolutionary search that generates diverse task sequences and control programs to explore beyond single-trajectory refinement. ASPIRE surpasses prior methods by up to 77% on LIBERO-Pro manipulation under perturbation, 72% on Robosuite bimanual handover, and 32% on BEHAVIOR-1K long-horizon household tasks. Its accumulated library also enables zero-shot generalization to unseen long-horizon tasks: on LIBERO-Pro Long, ASPIRE achieves 31% success versus 4% for prior methods despite their use of test-time reasoning and retries. Finally, simulation-discovered skills provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer, substantially reducing real-robot programming effort across different embodiments and robot APIs.

</details>


### [227] [What's Hidden Matters: Identifying Planning-Critical Occluded Agents using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00283)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Tyler Naes,Jovin D'sa,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Lifeng Zhou,David Isele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用规划KL散度（PKL）指标识别对自车轨迹影响最大的遮挡智能体，通过VLM生成结构化标注，训练模型聚焦规划关键遮挡区域，显著提升自动驾驶风险评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么对所有遮挡采用统一保守策略导致过度谨慎驾驶，要么推断隐藏空间但不评估其对规划的影响。需要弥合感知与规划之间的关键差距，识别对规划最关键的遮挡智能体。

Method: 提出PKL信息论指标系统识别和排序遮挡智能体，基于PKL排序使用GPT-5生成结构化标注，在nuScenes数据集构建高影响场景基准，对通用和领域自适应VLM进行微调训练。

Result: PKL引导的数据选择策略比随机采样提升约30%性能；小模型微调后显著超越大模型零样本性能；所有模型在PKL引导数据上微调均获得显著提升。

Conclusion: 首次提出系统化方法训练VLM聚焦规划关键遮挡，实现更语义化、高效的自动驾驶风险评估，填补了感知与规划间的关键空白。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must safely navigate complex environments where planning-critical agents may be hidden from view. Current approaches often treat all occlusions with uniform conservatism, yielding needlessly defensive driving, or they infer hidden spaces without estimating the impact on the planner. This work bridges the critical gap between perception and planning by enabling Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to identify and reason about the specific hidden agents that are most critical to the ego-vehicle's trajectory. We introduce a novel framework that uses Planning KL-divergence (PKL), an information-theoretic metric, to systematically identify and rank occluded agents based on their impact on the ego vehicle's plan. Using this planning-aware ranking, we employ an expert VLM (GPT-5) to generate rich, structured annotations that capture the visual evidence and reasoning required for this task. We apply this framework to the nuScenes dataset to create a new benchmark focused on high-impact scenarios. We conduct comprehensive experiments on a wide range of general-purpose and domain-adapted VLMs, demonstrating that fine-tuning on our PKL-guided data yields dramatic performance improvements across all models. Notably, our results show that smaller, fine-tuned models significantly outperform their much larger zero-shot counterparts, and that our PKL-guided data selection strategy improves performance by approximately 30\% over random sampling. Our work presents the first systematic approach for training VLMs to focus on planning-critical occlusions, enabling more semantically grounded and efficient risk assessment in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [228] [NeHMO: Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Learning for Decentralized Safe Multi-Arm Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00326)
*Qingyi Chen,Zachary Kingston,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经Hamilton-Jacobi可达性（HJR）学习的方法，通过近似安全值函数来捕捉最坏情况下的机械臂间安全约束，并构建了基于学习到的HJR表示的分布式轨迹优化框架，用于实时多臂运动规划。该方法具有可扩展性、数据高效性，能泛化到多种多机械臂系统，并在挑战性任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式多臂规划器可协调所有机械臂但可扩展性差，难以用于实时场景；分布式方法虽可扩展但依赖准确的行为预测或协调协议，当其他机械臂行为不可预测时可能失败。因此需要一种可扩展且鲁棒的分布式安全多臂运动规划方法。

Method: 1) 利用神经Hamilton-Jacobi可达性（HJR）学习来近似安全值函数，该函数捕捉最坏情况下的机械臂间安全约束；2) 基于学习到的HJR表示构建分布式轨迹优化框架，实现实时规划。

Result: 该方法具有可扩展性和数据高效性，能泛化到多种多机械臂系统，在挑战性多臂运动规划任务上优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 神经HJR学习方法有效解决了多臂运动规划中安全性与可扩展性的权衡问题，为分布式实时多机械臂安全规划提供了一种鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe multi-arm motion planning is a challenging problem in robotics due to its high dimensionality, coupled configuration space, and complex collision constraints. Centralized planners are capable of coordinating all arms but often face scalability limitations, restricting applicability in real-time settings. On the other hand, decentralized methods are scalable and recent deep learning-based approaches have shown promising results. However, these depend on accurate behavior prediction or coordination protocols and may fail when other arms act unpredictably. To address these challenges, we introduce a neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR) learning-based approach to approximate a safety value function that captures worst-case inter-arm safety constraints. We further develop a decentralized trajectory optimization framework that uses the learned HJR representation for real-time planning. The proposed method is scalable and data-efficient, generalizes across multi-manipulator systems, and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on challenging multi-arm motion planning tasks.

</details>


### [229] [Unleashing More Actions via Action Compositional Training for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00351)
*Kai Peng,Jie Lu,Xiaojiang Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型在机器人操控中因数据分布过拟合导致泛化能力差的问题，提出ACT-VLA框架，通过利用模型潜在任务表示从现有任务合成新演示数据，实现无需额外人工采集的数据增强，提升模型组合已知子技能的执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VLA训练范式导致模型严重过拟合于特定行为模式，即使仅需组合相同子技能的新场景也无法泛化。虽然扩大数据集可缓解过拟合，但高质量机器人数据采集成本高昂且耗时。

Method: 提出ACT-VLA（动作组合训练框架），是一种离线数据增强方法，利用模型的潜在任务表示从现有任务中自动合成新颖、物理上可行的演示数据，无需额外人工遥操作。

Result: 在仿真操控任务中，使用合成数据训练的策略相比基线VLA模型取得了显著更高的成功率，验证了自动演示合成的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用现有任务自动合成演示数据是扩展VLA模型泛化能力的一种有效、可扩展且数据高效的方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models excel at robotic manipulation, driven by the scale and diversity of demonstration data. However, standard training paradigms often cause VLA models to severely overfit to specific behavioral patterns, rendering them unable to generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios even when those scenarios merely require novel combinations of identical sub-skills. While expanding datasets can mitigate this overfitting, acquiring high-quality robot data remains notoriously labor-intensive and cost-prohibitive. To resolve this impasse without expensive human teleoperation and to truly unleash more actions,i.e., enable VLA models to compose known sub-skills into a much broader set of executable behaviors beyond the original demonstrations-we propose ACT-VLA (Action Compositional Training for VLA Models), an offline data augmentation framework that leverages the model's latent task representations to synthesize novel, physically valid demonstrations directly from existing tasks for policy training. By eliminating additional manual data collection, our method automatically expands the training distribution and mitigates overfitting. We evaluate our approach on challenging manipulation tasks in simulation. Experiments demonstrate that while baseline VLA models generalize poorly due to original distribution overfitting, policies trained with our synthesized data achieve substantially higher success rates, validating that leveraging existing tasks for automated demonstration synthesis provides an effective, scalable, and data-efficient route to broadening VLA generalization.

</details>


### [230] [Robust Operational Space Control with Conformal Disturbance Bounds for Safe Redundant Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00424)
*Wenhua Liu,Fan Zhang,Qin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合扩展状态观测器（ESO）与共形预测的鲁棒操作空间计算转矩控制框架，在无需全状态测量的条件下估计集总扰动，并通过滑窗共形预测在线估计扰动变化边界以实现实用概率安全保证。在7自由度Franka Research 3机械臂上验证了毫米级跟踪精度与1kHz实时安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统操作空间计算转矩控制（OSCTC）依赖精确动力学模型，在扰动下性能退化；而残差学习的数据驱动方法需要噪声全状态测量、缺乏严格理论保证且增加设计复杂度。现有鲁棒控制屏障函数（CBF）要求已知扰动变化边界以保证绝对安全，导致保守性过高。

Method: 1）在操作空间中直接使用ESO估计集总扰动，无需全状态测量；2）构建鲁棒CBF以在不确定性下保障安全；3）采用滑窗共形预测机制在线无分布地估计扰动变化边界，实现实用概率安全保证。

Result: 在7自由度Franka Research 3机械臂上，在多种扰动条件下实现毫米级跟踪精度与1kHz实时安全控制。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效融合了模型鲁棒性与数据驱动适应性，以共形预测替代保守的已知边界假设，在保证安全的前提下提升了跟踪性能，为冗余机械臂在约束和人机交互环境下的控制提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Redundant robotic manipulators operating in constrained and human-interactive environments require accurate task-space tracking together with rigorous safety guarantees under dynamic uncertainties. Classical operational space computed torque controller (OSCTC) relies on accurate dynamic models and degrades in the presence of disturbances. In contrast, the data-driven paradigm of residual learning approximates disturbances as functions learned from full-state measurements, which are often noisy in practice, lack rigorous theoretical guarantees, and introduce additional design complexity. This paper proposes a robust OSCTC framework that integrates an extended state observer (ESO) with conformal prediction to combine model-based robustness and data-driven adaptability.
