The ESO estimates lumped disturbances directly in operational space without requiring full-state measurements as in residual learning, and a robust control barrier function (CBF) is constructed to enforce safety under uncertainty. However, robust CBFs require a known disturbance-variation bound to guarantee absolute safety, which often leads to conservatism in practice. To address this limitation, we further employ a sliding-window conformal prediction mechanism to estimate the bound online in a distribution-free manner, thereby achieving practical probabilistic safety guarantees. Experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Research 3 manipulator demonstrate millimeter-level tracking accuracy and real-time safe control at 1~kHz under various disturbances.

</details>


### [231] [Learning Gait-Aware Quadruped Locomotion with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00442)
*Merve Atasever,Cagan Bakirci,Alfredo Reina Corona,Keyan Azbijari,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信号时序逻辑（STL）的奖励塑形框架，用于四足机器人步态控制。通过参数化STL约束定义三种步态（walking-trot、trot、bound），利用平滑鲁棒性计算生成稠密奖励，结合PPO算法在Barkour四足机器人上实现更紧的速度跟踪和更稳定的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人强化学习依赖固定、手工设计的马尔可夫奖励函数，导致学到的策略可解释性差且缺乏对步态行为的显式控制。本文旨在通过形式化规范（STL）实现可解释、可参数化的步态控制。

Method: 1) 使用参数化STL约束定义三种步态（行走-小跑、小跑、跳跃），包含安全边界、步态同步约束、命令跟踪和驱动边界。2) 基于STL规范设计奖励塑形机制，通过平滑近似STL鲁棒性计算稠密连续奖励。3) 在MuJoCo XLA中实例化Barkour四足机器人，利用并行化和域随机化加速训练并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 与手工设计的奖励基线相比，STL塑形奖励在Barkour四足机器人上实现了更紧的速度跟踪和更稳定的训练过程。

Conclusion: 基于STL的奖励塑形框架能够显式编码步态行为，提升策略的可解释性与控制能力，同时通过形式化规范简化奖励设计流程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for quadruped locomotion commonly depends on fixed, hand-crafted, and Markovian reward functions that limit both interpretability of learned policies and lack explicit control over gait behaviors. We introduce a framework where distinct gaits are specified using parameterized constraints expressed in Signal Temporal Logic (STL). These include safety bounds, gait synchronization constraints, command tracking, and actuation bounds. From these specifications, we develop a reward shaping mechanism that provides learning agents a dense, continuous reward landscape that encodes desired behavior. We define parametric STL templates for three speed regimes (walking-trot, trot, bound), calibrate their parameters from reference rollouts, and compute rewards from using smooth approximations of STL robustness over the rollouts. The generated rewards can be used to provide shaped gradients compatible with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). We instantiate the approach on Google's Barkour quadruped robot in MuJoCo XLA (MJX). We use parallelization within the simulator to improve training speeds and use domain randomization to robustify learned policies. We show that compared to a baseline of hand-crafted rewards, the STL-shaped rewards yield tighter velocity tracking and more stable training. Videos can be found on our project website: https://stl-locomotion.github.io/.

</details>


### [232] [Search-Based Spatiotemporal and Multi-Robot Motion Planning on Graphs of Space-Time Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00444)
*Jingtao Tang,Zining Mao,Lufan Yang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于时空凸集图(ST-GCS)的算法框架，通过将无碰撞区域表示为时空中的凸集，将轨迹规划转化为图搜索问题，并引入精确凸分解(ECD)处理动态障碍物和多机器人交互，在单机器人和多机器人问题中实现显著加速，可扩展到100台机器人的大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空运动规划，特别是在多机器人场景中，需要在连续空间中推理随时间变化的无碰撞区域，而可行区域往往是短暂存在且几何受限的，这使得问题极具挑战性。

Method: 提出ST-GCS框架，将无碰撞区域表示为时空凸集，将时间最优规划转化为基于路径索引状态的图搜索问题；开发最佳优先搜索求解器，结合可容许启发式和优势检查进行连续轨迹优化；提出精确凸分解(ECD)方案保留轨迹在时空中的占用；集成ST-GCS和ECD到优先级规划方法中，并引入窗口协调策略提升多机器人规划效率。

Result: 在单机器人和多机器人问题的广泛实验表明，该方法相较于多种规划器实现了显著加速，同时保持高求解质量，特别是在狭窄且短暂存在的可行区域环境中。大规模演示表明，该多机器人运动规划器可在几分钟内解决多达100台机器人的实例。

Conclusion: ST-GCS框架通过将连续时空运动规划问题离散化为结构化图搜索，有效处理了动态环境和多机器人场景中的时空约束，为复杂运动规划提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal motion planning, especially in multi-robot settings, requires robots to reason about collision-free regions that change over time, which is challenging in continuous spaces when feasible regions are transient and geometrically constrained. We present an algorithmic framework based on graphs of space-time convex sets (ST-GCSs), where collision-free regions are represented as convex sets in space-time and trajectories correspond to paths on the graph together with continuous motions within the selected sets. We formulate time-optimal planning on ST-GCSs as a graph-search problem over path-indexed states and develop a best-first search solver that evaluates partial paths via continuous trajectory optimization, guided by admissible heuristics and dominance checks. We further present an Exact Convex Decomposition (ECD) scheme to reserve trajectory occupancies in space-time, enabling unified handling of dynamic obstacles and multi-robot interactions. For multi-robot motion planning, we integrate ST-GCS planning and ECD into prioritized planning methods and introduce a windowed coordination scheme to improve efficiency. Extensive experiments on single-robot and multi-robot problems demonstrate substantial speedups over various planners while maintaining high solution quality, particularly in environments with narrow and transient feasible regions. Large-scale demonstrations further show that the proposed multi-robot motion planner can solve instances with up to $100$ robots within only a few minutes. Project homepage: https://sites.google.com/view/stgcs

</details>


### [233] [VLM-AR3L: Vision-Language Models for Absolute and Relative Rewards in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00483)
*Kuan-Chen Chen,Winston Chen,Wei-Fang Sun,Min-Chun Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLM-AR3L是一个利用视觉语言模型为强化学习生成绝对和相对奖励信号的框架，通过结合状态评估和比较监督，在经典控制、操作和开放世界具身任务中优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放环境中设计有效的奖励函数是强化学习的重大挑战，因为任务目标抽象且难以量化。

Method: VLM-AR3L利用VLM从偏好标签中学习绝对奖励模型（预测单个状态的标量评估）和相对奖励模型（比较连续观测以推断进展），并将两者集成以结合状态评估的稳定性和比较监督的鲁棒性。

Result: 在经典控制、操作和开放世界具身任务（特别是Minecraft）上的实验表明，VLM-AR3L consistently优于先前的VLM-based奖励学习方法。

Conclusion: VLM-AR3L通过将VLM生成的绝对和相对奖励信号相结合，有效解决了开放环境中奖励函数设计的难题，在视觉复杂和长时序决策任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in open-ended environments where task goals are abstract and difficult to quantify. In this work, we present VLM-AR3L, a framework that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide both absolute and relative rewards for RL. VLM-AR3L interprets an agent's visual observations in the context of a natural language task goal, and learns both absolute and relative rewards from VLM-generated preference labels. The absolute reward model predicts scalar evaluations for individual states, while the relative reward model compares consecutive observations to infer progress or regression toward the task goal. Their integration combines the stability of state-based evaluation with the robustness of comparative supervision. We evaluate VLM-AR3L across benchmarks spanning classic control, manipulation, and open-world embodied tasks, with a particular focus on Minecraft given its visual complexity and long-horizon decision-making requirements. Experimental results show that VLM-AR3L consistently outperforms prior VLM-based reward learning methods.

</details>


### [234] [From Technical Metrics to User Perception: A User Study of a Multimodal Human-Robot Interaction System for Object Detection and Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00530)
*Jian Song,Tian Zi,Shen Guanting*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过用户研究验证，将人机交互系统的端到端任务成功率从75%提升至90%的技术改进（替换感知和语言模块）能够被用户显著感知，且在偏好、速度、可靠性、流畅性和整体能力方面均具有统计学显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRI系统的技术性能提升不一定能被用户在实际交互中感知，因此需要验证技术改进是否在用户体验层面产生可测量的差异。

Method: 采用受试者内设计，24名参与者在桌面物体抓取任务中分别与基线系统（Whisper+Florence-2+LLaMA 3.1+区间二型模糊逻辑控制器）和改进系统（Grounding DINO+SAM+Qwen 3.5 9B+相同控制器）交互，使用7点李克特量表评估感知速度、可靠性、整体能力和流畅性。

Result: 70.83%的参与者偏好改进系统（精确二项检验，p=0.043，效应量h=0.43）；所有三个感知构念在改进系统上均显著更高（Holm校正后p<0.001），效应量大至极大。

Conclusion: 15个百分点的任务成功率提升足以产生用户可感知的差异，强调了在评估机器人操作流水线时需将基准测试与以用户为中心的评估相结合。

Abstract: Improvements in the technical performance of human--robot interaction (HRI) systems do not automatically translate into differences that human users can detect during live interaction. This paper investigates whether a 15 percentage point gain in end-to-end task success (from 75% in a multimodal baseline system to 90% in an improved configuration identified through a prior ablation study) is sufficient to produce consistent and measurable differences in user perception. The baseline system combines Whisper for speech recognition, Florence-2 for open-vocabulary object detection, LLaMA 3.1 for action extraction, and an interval Type-2 fuzzy logic controller for motion execution. The improved configuration replaces the perception and language modules with Grounding DINO + SAM and Qwen 3.5 9B, respectively, while retaining the same controller. A within-subject user study with 24 participants compared both systems on the same tabletop object-grasping task. After interacting with each configuration, participants rated perceived speed, reliability, and overall competence and fluency on a 7-point Likert scale. Results show that 17 out of 24 participants (70.83%) preferred the improved system (exact binomial test, p = 0.043, h = 0.43), and all three perceptual constructs were rated significantly higher for the improved configuration after Holm correction, with large to very large effect sizes (p < 0.001). These findings confirm that the identified technical improvements are perceptible to users in direct interaction and underscore the importance of complementing benchmark evaluation with user-centred evidence when assessing robotic manipulation pipelines.

</details>


### [235] [Learning from Demonstration via Spatiotemporal Tubes for Unknown Euler-Lagrange Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00534)
*Ratnangshu Das,Puneeth Shankar,Varuni Buereddy,Ravi Prakash,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STT-LfD框架，利用异方差高斯过程学习时空管作为任务意图包络，并设计闭环反馈控制器在考虑执行器限制下强制执行所学约束，无需显式系统辨识，在移动机器人和7自由度机械臂上验证了优越的鲁棒性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统从示教学习方法将运动学习与控制解耦，仅跟踪固定参考轨迹，无法处理未知欧拉-拉格朗日系统中对扰动的鲁棒性问题，且需要显式系统辨识。

Method: 将示教视为数据驱动的安全规范，通过异方差高斯过程学习时空管作为任务意图包络，捕捉任务的时间变精度要求；设计封闭形式的反馈控制器强制执行所学约束，同时考虑执行器限制，无需系统辨识。

Result: 在移动机器人和7自由度机械臂上的硬件实验中，该方法在扰动鲁棒性和计算速度方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: STT-LfD是一种统一的学习从示教框架，保持示教的时间结构、计算高效、无需系统辨识，且具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present STT-LfD, a unified Learning from Demonstration (LfD) framework that integrates motion learning with control for unknown Euler-Lagrange systems. Unlike traditional decoupled approaches that track a fixed reference, the proposed method treats demonstrations as a data-driven safety specification. Using heteroscedastic Gaussian Processes, STT-LfD learns Spatiotemporal Tubes (STTs) as an intent envelope that capture time-varying precision requirements of a task. A closed-form feedback controller then enforces these learned constraints while respecting actuator limits, without requiring explicit system identification. The approach preserves the temporal structure of demonstrations, remains computationally efficient, and avoids explicit system identification. Hardware experiments on a mobile robot and a 7-DOF manipulator show that it outperforms baselines in robustness to disturbances and computational speed.

</details>


### [236] [[Preprint] Dynamic Modeling, Gait Synthesis, and Control of a Novel Subsurface Bore Propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00569)
*Lina van Brügge,Shruti Kotpalliwar,Anton Koval,Akshit Saradagi,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化地下钻探机器人，模仿蚯蚓的锚定-推进运动和隧道掘进机的挖掘原理，通过Euler-Lagrange动力学建模、集中式状态机步态合成和反馈控制，在Unity物理仿真和ROS集成中验证了机器人三个步态周期内30mm的前进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无需人工干预的地下探索与挖掘任务，解决传统地下探测设备在复杂地下环境中自主推进和掘进的难题。

Method: 采用五模块解耦设计（钻头+2锚定模块+2推进模块），基于Euler-Lagrange框架建立动力学模型，设计解耦反馈控制器，通过集中式状态机实现步态合成；使用Unity物理引擎（基于CAD模型）和ROS进行仿真验证。

Result: 在物理仿真中，机器人完成3个步态周期后，成功实现锚定并在土壤中产生30mm的总前进位移。

Conclusion: 所提出的设计、控制器和步态合成策略能够有效实现机器人在土壤中的锚定与推进，验证了仿生地下钻探机器人的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: In this article, we present dynamic modeling, gait synthesis, and feedback control design for a modular novel subsurface robot, designed for human-free subsurface exploration and excavation. The subsurface propagator design is based on two major aspects: 1) anchor and propel movement like an earthworm and 2) excavation similar to tunnel boring machines. This design is decoupled into five separate modules: one drill head to excavate and create cavity for propagation, two modules to anchor the robot, and two modules to enable propagation of the body. In order to design a controller for each of the modules, dynamic models using the Euler-Lagrange framework are developed. These mathematical models are used as a baseline to design controlled decoupled operation of the different joint movements. The operation of robotic assembly is constructed via a centralized state machine for gait synthesis with integration of the designed feedback controller. The controllers are tested on the real robot geometry to aid sim-to-real integration: A physics-based Unity simulation using a CAD model of the robot and integration of the trained controller via ROS verifies the performance of the robot. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed design, controllers and the gait synthesis strategy together are capable of anchoring the robot in place and creating an total advancement of 30\,mm into the soil after completing 3 gait cycles.

</details>


### [237] [Enhancing Robustness in Robot-Environment Interactions through Passive Compliant Degrees of Freedom: A Hybrid Position-Force Control Approach with Feedback Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00571)
*Rahman Ardakanian,Iman Kardan,AliAkbar Akbari,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合反馈线性化混合位力控制与末端被动柔顺自由度（弹簧-阻尼器）的交互架构，用于减弱机器人动态/非结构化环境中的冲击振荡和接触不确定性。在2-DOF平面机械臂上的仿真表明，弹簧-阻尼器配置相比刚性或纯弹簧配置显著降低了接触振荡、力/速度误差方差和关节扭矩波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态或非结构化环境中，机器人-环境交互常因冲击、振动以及接触几何与力学特性的不确定性而性能下降，传统混合位力控制依赖主动反馈、力传感和增益调节，难以有效抑制高频冲击。

Method: 提出一种交互架构，将反馈线性化混合位力控制与末端执行器处嵌入的被动柔顺自由度（弹簧-阻尼器）相结合。物理弹簧-阻尼器界面在接触点储存和耗散冲击能量，防止高频冲击传播至驱动关节和力控制回路。在MATLAB/Simulink中，对2-DOF平面机械臂分别采用刚性、纯弹簧和弹簧-阻尼器三种末端配置进行仿真评估。

Result: 在固定和时变交互条件下，弹簧-阻尼器配置在接触振荡衰减、力与速度误差方差降低以及关节扭矩响应平滑性方面均优于其他配置。具体数据：固定环境下切向力误差标准差降低36.5%，变化环境下法向力误差标准差降低25.4%，变化环境下法向速度误差标准差降低41.1%。

Conclusion: 被动柔顺自由度（弹簧-阻尼器）与混合位力控制结合可有效提升机器人在动态/非结构化环境中的交互稳定性与精度，减少对主动反馈和增益调节的依赖。

Abstract: Robot-environment interactions in dynamic or unstructured settings are often degraded by impact shocks, vibrations, and uncertainties in contact geometry and mechanical properties. This paper proposes an interaction architecture that combines feedback-linearized hybrid position-force control with a passive compliant degree of freedom embedded at the end-effector. Unlike conventional hybrid position-force control, which relies mainly on active feedback, force sensing, and gain tuning, the proposed architecture uses a physical spring-damper interface to store and dissipate impact energy at the contact point before high-frequency shocks propagate to the actuated joints and force-control loop. The approach is evaluated in MATLAB/Simulink on a 2-DOF planar manipulator with three end-effector configurations: rigid, spring-only, and spring-damper. Results under fixed and time-varying interaction conditions show that the spring-damper configuration provides stronger attenuation of contact-induced oscillations, lower force and velocity error variance, and smoother joint-torque response. Representative reductions include 36.5% in fixed-environment tangential force-error standard deviation, 25.4% in variable-environment normal force-error standard deviation, and 41.1% in variable-environment normal velocity-error standard deviation.

</details>


### [238] [From Real-Time Planning to Reliable Execution:Scalable Coordination for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Fleets in Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00591)
*Bo Cao,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE，一个反应式在线协调框架，通过运动诱导冲突消减机制和广义共轭动作-优先超图（CAPH），实现异构机器人集群在工业环境中的实时路径规划与鲁棒执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着异构机器人车队在工业环境中的部署增加，实时路径规划面临高密度、异构运动能力、通信延迟和执行不确定性等挑战，导致机器人偏离计划路径，引发过度等待和拥堵传播。

Method: 提出SCALE反应式在线协调框架，包含运动诱导冲突消减机制以支持在线可行路径生成与冲突解决，以及广义共轭动作-优先超图（CAPH）自适应调整机器人间的优先级关系以缓解扰动影响。

Result: 通过大量验证实验及在仓库中为期三天的实际部署验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SCALE框架能够实现异构机器人集群的实时规划与鲁棒执行，有效应对通信延迟和执行不确定性带来的扰动问题。

Abstract: With the increasing deployment of heterogeneous robot fleets in industrial environments, efficient coordination remains a critical challenge. Real-time path planning must simultaneously accommodate high robot densities and heterogeneous motion capabilities, while communication delays, execution uncertainties, and other disturbances may cause robots to deviate from the temporal assumptions underlying planned paths. Such deviations can lead to excessive waiting and congestion propagation across the fleet. This paper presents SCALE, a reactive online coordination framework that enables real-time planning while maintaining robust execution. Within this framework, we introduce a motion-induced conflict reduction mechanism to support the online generation of feasible paths for online conflict resolution. To mitigate the effects of disturbances, we further design a generalized Conjugate Action-Precedence Hypergraph (CAPH) that adaptively adjusts precedence relations among robots. Extensive validation experiments, together with a three-day deployment in a warehouse, demonstrate the

</details>


### [239] [Domain Arithmetic: One-Shot VLA Adaptation under Environmental Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00666)
*Taewook Kang,Taeheon Kim,Donghyun Shin,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART是一种基于类比权重视图运算的VLA模型领域自适应方法，仅需单次演示即可在环境变化（如相机视角、机器人类型）下高效适配，通过子空间对齐过滤噪声成分，在仿真和真实实验中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在环境变化（相机位姿、机器人类型等）下性能显著下降，而传统适配方法需要大量任务演示数据，收集成本高昂。因此需要一种高效、低数据依赖的领域自适应方法。

Method: 提出Domain ARiThmetic (DART)，基于类比推理的权重视图运算方法：(1) 仅需收集单个目标领域演示；(2) 通过子空间对齐对权重向量中的奇异成分进行对齐，过滤噪声成分；(3) 添加领域特定信息到源领域权重向量中实现适配。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，DART在一次性（one-shot）场景下，面对视觉偏移（相机视角变化）和具身偏移（不同机器人）均优于现有的VLA适配方法。

Conclusion: DART通过权重视图运算和子空间对齐，以极低的数据成本（单次演示）实现了VLA模型在环境变化下的高效适配，为机器人策略的快速迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to perform the same learned tasks under environmental shifts, such as changes in camera pose and shifts to a different but similar robot (e.g., from Panda to UR5e). Adapting these models to the shifted environment (i.e., target domain) often requires training on multiple demonstrations for each task, which are costly to collect. To reduce the burden of data curation and training, we propose an analogy-based method that adapts VLA models under environmental shifts through weight vector arithmetic with domain-specific information addition, named Domain ARiThmetic (DART). Unlike prior approaches, DART requires collecting only a single demonstration, enabling efficient adaptation. To accurately isolate domain-specific information for addition, DART performs subspace alignment between singular components in weight vectors to filter out noisy components. In both simulated and real-world experiments, DART outperforms existing VLA adaptation methods in one-shot scenarios across diverse visual and embodiment shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/snumprlab/dart.

</details>


### [240] [Path Planning in Physically Viable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00673)
*Su Ann Low,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Xingjian Li,Adam J. Thorpe,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理可行的世界模型，用于评估机器人导航在长期地形变化下的路径可行性。该模型通过将3D高斯泼溅场景与物理仿真结合，生成地形变化后的虚拟环境，并实现地形感知规划器，使机器人能在部署前评估未来地形变化对路径的影响。实验在真实野外场地验证了该方法在暴露路径失效和重新规划行为方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化户外环境中通常使用部署前采集的静态地图进行长期规划，但这些地图无法反映执行时环境发生的物理变化（如洪水、滑坡等），导致原本可行的路径可能变得不安全或不可达。现有方法缺乏对地形物理变化的前瞻性评估能力。

Method: 1) 构建物理可行的世界模型：将重建的3D高斯泼溅场景与基于物理的仿真结合，生成环境的地形变化版本，无需重新采集传感器数据或重建地图。2) 实现地形感知规划器：考虑物理事件、障碍物和变形，评估规划路径在变化后的可行性。3) 在德克萨斯州中部真实野外场地，模拟不同严重程度的洪水进行验证。

Result: 物理可行的世界模型能够暴露仅基于原始重建环境规划时无法发现的长期路径失效和重新规划行为。随着物理模拟干预下地形条件恶化，系统能有效评估路径和任务可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法使机器人能在部署前评估未来地形变化对路径可行性的影响，特别适用于约束环境中撤退或恢复可能变得不可能的场景，提高了导航安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robots deployed in unstructured outdoor environments often plan from scene reconstructions collected before deployment because operators cannot remap large or remote sites before every mission. As a result, robots must make long-horizon planning decisions using stale maps that assume the terrain remains unchanged, even though physical changes to the environment may render previously feasible routes unsafe or unreachable at execution time. We present a physically viable world model for evaluating what-if queries for robot navigation under future terrain change. The system augments reconstructed 3D Gaussian splat scenes with physics-based simulation to generate physically modified versions of the same environment without recollecting sensor data or rebuilding the map. We then implement a terrain-aware planner that accounts for physical events, obstacles, and deformations that are simulated by the world model. This allows robots and human operators to evaluate whether planned routes remain feasible before committing to a planned route, particularly in constrained environments where retreat or recovery may become impossible once conditions change. We evaluate the system on a real outdoor field site in Central Texas using simulated flooding across multiple severity levels. We measure route and mission feasibility as terrain conditions deteriorate under physically simulated interventions. Our results show that physically viable world models expose long-horizon route failures and rerouting behavior that are not apparent when planning only on the original reconstructed environment, allowing robots to evaluate how future terrain changes may affect route feasibility before deployment.

</details>


### [241] [From Prediction Uncertainty to Conformalized Distance Fields for Safe Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00776)
*Jaeuk Shin,Yoonseok Ra,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出功能共形预测（FCP）框架，通过对整个预测距离场进行共形校准，得到分布自由、场级别的安全下界，嵌入采样模型预测控制器（FCP-MPC），在ETH-UCY行人基准和密集3D四旋翼任务中，相比逐点和自我中心共形基线，实现了更优的安全-可行性-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形方法对每个障碍物的预测误差聚合为标量进行校准，丢失了空间连贯性且随场景密度扩展性差，无法在动态环境中兼顾实时性能与不确定性推理。

Method: 提出功能共形预测（FCP），对整个残差距离场进行共形校准；利用残差距离场的经验低秩性和近似时间不变性，在系数空间分解下界；离线通过功能PCA和高斯混合归纳共形过程拟合包络，在线通过轻量自适应功能共形（AFCP）更新低维向量，嵌入采样模型预测控制器（FCP-MPC）。

Result: 在ETH-UCY行人基准和含多达280个动态障碍物的密集3D四旋翼任务中，FCP-MPC在安全性、可行性和效率间取得有利平衡，成功到达目标点，而逐点和自我中心共形基线过于保守或计算昂贵，且每步计算远低于在线不确定性推理基线。

Conclusion: FCP框架通过场级别的共形预测，实现了与障碍物数量无关的实时安全约束，在动态密集场景中兼具理论保证和实际效率，为运动规划中的不确定性推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in dynamic environments requires reasoning about the uncertainty in predicted obstacle motion without sacrificing real-time performance. Existing conformal approaches conformalize a scalar score that aggregates per-obstacle prediction errors, losing spatial coherence and scaling poorly with scene density. We instead conformalize the entire predicted distance field at once. This functional conformal prediction (FCP) framework yields a distribution-free, field-level lower bound, from which safety follows uniformly: any trajectory satisfying the resulting constraint is certified safe, independent of how the control space is sampled. The key enabler is that the residual distance field is empirically low-rank and approximately time-invariant, which makes the bound decomposable in coefficient space. An envelope is fitted offline via functional PCA and a Gaussian-mixture inductive conformal procedure, then refined online by a lightweight adaptive functional conformal (AFCP) update on a low-dimensional vector. This keeps the per-step cost largely insensitive to obstacle count and retains long-run field coverage under distribution shift. We embed the envelope as a tightened safety constraint in a sampling-based model predictive controller, FCP-MPC. On the ETH--UCY pedestrian benchmarks and a dense 3D quadrotor task with up to 280 dynamic obstacles, FCP-MPC attains a favorable balance of safety, feasibility, and efficiency, reaching goals where pointwise and egocentric conformal baselines become too conservative or too expensive, while keeping per-step computation far below online uncertainty-reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [242] [From World Models to World Action Models: A Concise Tutorial for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00836)
*Xiaoxiong Zhang,Xiong Zeng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型的设计空间视图，将其定义为动作条件预测模型，并系统分类为观测空间和状态空间模型，同时引入世界动作模型，总结四种代表性范式，为具身预测与控制提供结构化的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在具身智能和生成式仿真中的范围定义模糊，不同社区对其理解不一致，需要澄清概念范围并提供结构化分类。

Method: 采用设计空间视图，将世界模型定义为动作条件预测模型；分为观测空间和状态空间两类并比较权衡；引入世界动作模型，总结四种范式：先想象后执行、视频特征条件动作预测、联合视频-动作建模、辅助视频预测用于策略学习。

Result: 构建了世界模型的结构化分类体系，明确了观测空间与状态空间模型的视觉保真度、空间结构、物理解释性和控制可用性权衡，归纳了四种世界动作模型范式。

Conclusion: 该教程澄清了世界（动作）模型的概念范围，为具身预测与控制研究提供了系统化的分类框架。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in embodied intelligence and generative simulation, yet their scope remains ambiguous across communities. This tutorial presents a design-space view of world models as action-conditioned predictive models that estimate the future evolution of task-relevant observations or states. We categorize existing methods into observation-space and state-space world models, comparing their trade-offs in visual fidelity, spatial structure, physical interpretability, and control usability. We further introduce world action models, which connect predicted futures with executable robot actions, and summarize four representative paradigms: imagine-then-execute, video-feature-conditioned action prediction, joint video-action modeling, and auxiliary video prediction for policy learning. The goal of this tutorial is to clarify the conceptual scope of world (action) models and provide a structured taxonomy for embodied prediction and control.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Line of Sight: Hybrid Validation of V2X Collective Perception in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00874)
*Markos Antonopoulos,Anastasia Bolovinou,Bill Roungas,Elena Daskalaki,Angelos Amditis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向V2X集体感知的概率框架与混合验证方法，通过贝叶斯融合算法将多智能体异构传感器观测整合为共享概率占据网格，在环岛场景中实现了260%的视野扩展和占据单元召回率从0.82提升至0.94，为协同自动驾驶的安全部署提供了可复现、可解释的验证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展单车感知范围有限的问题，为V2X集体感知提供可解释、可信赖且可复现的验证方法，弥合仿真与真实测试之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出贝叶斯融合算法，将多智能体异构传感器观测融合为共享概率占据格网，每个单元编码占据似然与不确定性；开发混合测试框架，结合CARLA虚拟环境与车辆在环实验。

Result: 在环岛场景中，视野覆盖范围提升260%；占据单元召回率从单车0.82提升至六智能体协同下的0.94（标称定位条件下）。

Conclusion: 该方法为CP系统验证提供了可复现、可解释的基础，支持协同自动驾驶的安全与可认证部署。

Abstract: This paper introduces a probabilistic framework and hybrid validation methodology for V2X-enabled Collective Perception (CP) in complex traffic scenarios. The proposed Bayesian fusion algorithm extends the perceptual horizon of connected and autonomous vehicles by integrating heterogeneous sensor observations from multiple agents into a shared probabilistic occupancy grid. Each cell of this grid encapsulates both occupancy likelihood and uncertainty, enabling explainable and trustworthy situational awareness beyond the ego vehicle's field of view. To bridge the gap between simulation and real-world evaluation, a hybrid testing framework is developed, combining CARLA-based virtual environments with vehicle-in-the-loop experimentation. Experimental results in a roundabout scenario demonstrate a 260 percent increase in field-of-view coverage and a rise in occupied-cell recall from 0.82 (ego-only) to 0.94 (six-agent CP) under nominal localization conditions. Overall, the proposed approach provides a reproducible and interpretable foundation for validating CP systems, supporting the safe and certifiable deployment of cooperative autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [244] [AMBUSH: Collaborative Capture in Complex Environments with Neural Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01029)
*Junfeng Chen,YinHang Luo,Xinyi Wang,Junrui Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"伏击"策略的参数化方法，结合混合蒙特卡洛树搜索（H-MCTS）与神经网络加速，使多个慢速追踪者在复杂环境中高效捕获一个快速逃避者，并在仿真和硬件实验中验证了对不同智能水平逃避者的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中弱小物种常通过协作捕获动态目标，类似概念对机器人安全监控、搜索救援等应用至关重要。但现有方法多限于无障碍或障碍稀疏分布的环境，缺乏在复杂环境中用慢速追踪者捕获快速逃避者的有效方案。

Method: 首先设计参数化伏击策略（包含离散/连续参数），考虑工作空间拓扑特性、截断视线可见性、相对速度比和有限捕获范围；然后提出H-MCTS算法通过长期规划优化相关参数；最后离线训练神经网络学习不同环境下参数选择的排序并直接预测得分，替代H-MCTS中的rollout过程，在线加速规划并保证质量。

Result: 在广泛仿真和硬件实验中，该方法有效捕获了具有不同能力和智能水平的逃避者，包括速度高出两倍以及人类控制的逃避者。

Conclusion: 仅靠"伏击"策略就足以让多个慢速追踪者在复杂环境中高效捕获不同智能水平的快速逃避者，H-MCTS结合神经网络加速提供了高效的长期规划方案。

Abstract: Collaborative capture of dynamic targets is common in nature as an essential strategy for weaker species against the strong. Similar concepts have shown to be useful for numerous robotic applications, such as security and surveillance, search and rescue. However, most existing works focus on analytical and geometric solutions or end-to-end reinforcement learning methods, which are largely constrained to obstacle-free environments or scenarios with sparse, regularly distributed obstacles. This work tackles the problem from a unique perspective: the renowned strategy of``ambush'' alone would suffice for multiple slower pursuers to capture one faster evader with different levels of intelligence efficiently in complex environments. A parameterized strategy of ambush (including discrete and continuous parameters) is designed first, which takes into account the topological properties of the workspace, the truncated line-of-sight visibility, the relative speed ratio and the limited capture range. Then, a Hybrid Monte Carlo Tree Search (H-MCTS) algorithm is proposed to optimize the associated parameters through long-term planning, enabling the identification of highly promising parameters for future capture. Lastly, the neural acceleration is trained offline to learn the ranking of different choices of parameters across various environments, and to directly predict scores, replacing the rollout process in H-MCTS. The neural acceleration is adopted during online H-MCTS to accelerate the planning procedure while guaranteeing the planning quality. Its efficiency and effectiveness are validated in extensive simulations and hardware experiments, against evaders with different capabilities and intelligence levels, including two-times higher velocity and human-controlled behavior.

</details>


### [245] [DART-VLN: Test-Time Memory Decay and Anti-Loop Regularization for Discrete Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01043)
*Shaoheng Zhang,Zhichen Li,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-VLN提出了一种无需训练、测试时控制的框架，通过记忆衰减和防循环正则化，在不修改模型参数的情况下提升离散视觉语言导航的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的离散VLN智能体在测试时存在两个主要问题：1）记忆读取时历史证据过时；2）动作选择时低效的局部回溯。这些问题即使在强冻结backbone下也无法避免。

Method: 提出DART-VLN框架，包含两个核心组件：1）测试时记忆衰减（Test-Time Memory Decay），一种读取侧的记忆重加权规则，在不修改存储内容的情况下抑制过时和冗余证据；2）防循环正则化（Anti-Loop Regularization），一种轻量的下一跳惩罚机制，在动作选择时抑制立即反转。无需引入新可学习参数，也不改变已学习的骨干网络。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE数据集上：仅使用衰减即可获得稳定的读取侧增益；衰减+防循环实现最佳的质量-效率权衡，包括更短的轨迹、更低的运行时以及在关键设置下提升的导航性能。行为分析证实防循环正则化减少了局部回溯并提高了路径效率。

Conclusion: 适度的测试时控制可以在无需重新训练的情况下，使基于记忆的离散VLN更加可靠和高效。

Abstract: Memory-based discrete vision-language navigation (VLN) agents must act under partial observability, yet even strong frozen backbones remain vulnerable at test time. Two common failure modes are stale historical evidence at memory readout and inefficient local backtracking during action selection. We present DART-VLN, a training-free test-time control framework for discrete VLN. DART-VLN combines Test-Time Memory Decay, a read-side memory reweighting rule that suppresses stale and redundant evidence without rewriting stored content, with Anti-Loop Regularization, a lightweight next-hop penalty that discourages immediate reversals during action selection. The framework introduces no new learnable parameters and leaves the learned backbone unchanged. Experiments on R2R and REVERIE show a consistent pattern: decay-only provides stable read-side gains, while decay+anti-loop achieves the best overall quality-efficiency trade-off, yielding shorter trajectories, lower runtime, and improved navigation performance in key settings. Behavioral analysis further confirms that anti-loop regularization reduces local backtracking and improves path efficiency under frozen backbones. Overall, the results show that modest test-time control can make memory-based discrete VLN more reliable and efficient without retraining.

</details>


### [246] [Robots Ask the Way: Communication-Enabled Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01044)
*Valentino Sacco,Luca Scofano,Indro Spinelli,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通信增强型社交导航（CommNav），让机器人在多人类环境中主动向居民询问目标人物信息，并结合Habitat 3.0c仿真平台验证，通信模块使任务成功率提升10个百分点，且对自然语言指令具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航方法仅依赖反应式碰撞避免和轨迹调整，缺乏通过人机通信主动获取信息的机制，导致在多智能体环境中难以高效定位特定目标个体。

Method: 提出CommNav任务，让机器人主动向居民请求目标人物的目击信息、位置和行动轨迹；扩展Habitat 3.0为Habitat 3.0c以支持多人类环境下的信息交换协议；设计通信模块（COMM）集成到现有社交导航模型；分别使用LLM生成的指令和人类口语化指令进行训练与评估。

Result: COMM模块使社交导航模型的Episode Success提升10个百分点；基于通信预训练任务可有效应对偶发交互信号；使用自然口语化语言时任务成功率与使用完美结构化数据时统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 显式人机通信能显著提升多目标社交导航性能；通信预训练是解决交互信号稀疏问题的有效策略；导航策略对自然语言具有高度鲁棒性。

Abstract: Assistive autonomous robots operating in multi-agent environments require efficient strategies to locate specific individuals among multiple residents. Current social navigation methods focus on reactive collision avoidance and trajectory adaptation, but lack mechanisms to proactively gather information through human-robot communication.
  We introduce Communication-enabled Social Navigation (CommNav). In this novel task, robotic agents actively seek assistance from residents to locate target individuals by requesting information about recent sightings, locations, and movements.
  To evaluate CommNav, we extend Habitat 3.0 to create Habitat 3.0c, a communication-enabled variant supporting multi-human environments with information exchange protocols. Adding our communication module (COMM) to a state-of-the-art social navigation model yields a 10 percentage-point improvement in Episode Success. We further investigate the transition from structured data to natural language by evaluating models trained on LLM-generated instructions and on colloquial instructions collected from a human study.
  Our experiments reveal that: (i) explicit human-robot communication substantially enhances multi-person navigation performance; (ii) pre-training COMM on a communication pretext task effectively addresses the challenge of occasional interaction signals; and (iii) the navigation policy is highly robust to natural, colloquial human language, achieving an episode success statistically similar to the model using perfect structured data.

</details>


### [247] [AutoSpeed: Annotation-Free Stage-Adaptive Motion Speed Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01051)
*Qingda Hu,Ziheng Qiu,Jieru Zhao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AutoSpeed框架，通过频域速度调制实现操作任务中与阶段自适应运动速度，无需额外标注，在缩短执行时间的同时提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿学习的视觉运动策略通常以固定速度模仿专家演示，无法根据不同操作阶段（简单/复杂）自适应调整执行速度和时序预测范围，限制了灵活性和整体任务吞吐量。

Method: 提出AutoSpeed框架：将不同速度下的未来轨迹视为候选优化目标，用复合代价（权衡预测误差和预测范围）评估各候选，选择代价最小的候选优化策略；通过离散余弦变换（DCT）在频域实现平滑、非整数倍的速度缩放，实现固定长度动作序列下对不同阶段自适应调整有效时间预测范围。

Result: 大量实验表明AutoSpeed显著减少了任务执行时间，同时提升了任务成功率；推断出的运动速度与任务阶段高度对应。

Conclusion: AutoSpeed是一种与模型无关的框架，能使现有视觉运动策略具备阶段自适应的运动速度，无需速度或阶段标注，兼顾效率与成功率。

Abstract: Different stages of manipulation tasks exhibit varying levels of difficulty, suggesting stage-dependent motion speeds and temporal prediction horizons. However, existing IL-based visuomotor policies typically imitate the execution speed of expert demonstrations and operate with a fixed temporal prediction horizon, limiting flexibility and overall task throughput. In this paper, we introduce AutoSpeed, a model-agnostic learning framework that enables existing visuomotor policies to predict trajectories with stage-adaptive motion speeds, without requiring speed or stage annotations. We treat future trajectories at different speeds as candidate optimization targets, evaluate each candidate using a composite cost that trades off prediction error against prediction horizon, and optimize the policy toward the minimum-cost candidate. With a fixed-length action sequence, speed modulation adjusts the effective temporal prediction horizon: simple stages are executed faster with a longer prediction horizon, whereas complex stages are executed more slowly with a shorter prediction horizon. Specifically, we implement speed modulation in the frequency domain via the discrete cosine transform (DCT), which enables smooth, non-integer speed scaling and thus preserves motion continuity. Extensive evaluations show that AutoSpeed substantially reduces task execution time while also improving success rates. Under the AutoSpeed framework, the inferred motion speeds exhibit a strong correspondence with task stages.

</details>


### [248] [RoboWorld: Fast and Reliable Neural Simulators for Generalist Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01060)
*Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,JaeHyeok Doo,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWorld 是一个自动化评估管线，通过 Step Forcing 技术结合自回归视频世界模型与任务感知视觉语言模型评分器，实现与真实世界机器人评估的高一致性（Pearson r=0.989, Spearman ρ=0.970）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型作为评估通用机器人策略的可扩展替代方案面临两大挑战：世界模型误差导致生成的 rollout 不可靠，以及推理速度慢限制大规模吞吐量。现有方法难以同时解决可靠性和效率问题。

Method: 提出 RoboWorld 自动化评估管线：(1) 快速自回归视频世界模型生成 rollout；(2) Step Forcing 技术结合 anchored context 与一步自回归上下文，减少训练-测试不匹配同时保持动作-观测动态；(3) 任务进度感知的视觉语言模型评分器评估 rollout 质量。

Result: 在跨任务和跨环境设置下，RoboWorld 与真实世界机器人评估高度对齐，Pearson 相关系数 r=0.989，Spearman 秩相关系数 ρ=0.970。

Conclusion: RoboWorld 通过 Step Forcing 解决了自回归世界模型 rollout 的可靠性问题，实现了高效且可靠的机器人策略自动化评估，可作为真实世界部署的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Video world models are emerging as a scalable alternative for evaluating generalist robot policies, bypassing the physical constraints and engineering burdens of real-world deployment. However, evaluating policies with video world models remains challenging, as world-model errors can make generated rollouts unreliable and slow inference limits large-scale throughput. We introduce RoboWorld, an automated evaluation pipeline that pairs a fast autoregressive video world model with a task-progress-aware vision-language model scoring. To enable reliable long-horizon autoregressive world-model rollouts, we propose Step Forcing, which combines anchored and one-step self-forwarded contexts to reduce train--test mismatch while preserving action--observation dynamics. Together, these components enable RoboWorld to align strongly with real-world robot evaluation across tasks and environments, achieving Pearson's r = 0.989 and Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.970.

</details>


### [249] [Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01067)
*Chi Zhang,Penglin Cai,Ziheng Xi,Haoqi Yuan,Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Sipeng Zheng,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模触觉-动作数据集H-Tac（160小时、300+任务、13.5万条人类演示片段），并在此基础上构建可迁移触觉预训练框架TTP，通过统一触觉与动作空间的人类到机器人迁移学习，利用触觉专家模型进行未来触觉预测，显著提升了机器人细粒度操作和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉数据集规模小、接触覆盖窄，且VLA模型受限于缺乏动力学感知的后训练，制约了下游任务性能上限。

Method: 构建大规模触觉-动作数据集H-Tac；提出TTP框架，在预训练和后训练阶段使用统一的触觉与动作空间以保持迁移时的先验知识；利用触觉专家模型进行未来触觉预测，显式建模接触动力学和物理交互。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人上均取得优异性能，展现出鲁棒的泛化能力和细粒度操作能力。

Conclusion: TTP为通过人类到机器人迁移实现可扩展的触觉预训练铺平了道路。

Abstract: As an essential modality for dexterous and contact-rich tasks, tactile sensing provides precise force feedback that cannot be reliably inferred from vision. However, limited by hardware and data collection systems, existing datasets with tactility remain small in scale and narrow in contact coverage. Meanwhile, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with tactile modality are constrained on dynamics-agnostic post-training, which limits the performance ceiling on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset with 160-hour egocentric human videos containing more than 300 tasks and 135k episodes. Building upon this, we propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a system of tactile-based pre-training on human data for fine-grained robotic tasks. To bridge the gap between humans and robots, we use unified tactile and action spaces throughout the pre-training and post-training phases, preserving prior knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By leveraging a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, our framework explicitly models the contact dynamics and precise physical interactions. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance, exhibiting robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities. TTP paves the way for scalable tactile pre-training via human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [250] [Where Am I? Semantic Map Grounding via Vision-Language Models for Multi-Modal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01079)
*Suraj Borate,Aarav Shah,Madhu Vadali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器人定位重构为语义推理任务，基于视觉-语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B + LoRA微调）直接预测连续位姿，在自定义Gazebo数据集上达到96.75%的完整位姿准确率，并展现出跨模态互补性、泛化到未见物体类别及适应不完整地图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS缺失的室内环境中，传统几何估计方法存在局限性，受人类利用物体级线索和标注地图进行定位的启发，探索将定位问题转化为语义推理任务，利用视觉-语言模型实现端到端位姿预测。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B作为骨干网络，通过LoRA微调并附加轻量级回归头，从最终隐藏状态直接预测连续位姿坐标(x, y, theta)。采用复合位置-方向损失函数和课程学习策略，在包含120,112个样本、527个场景的自定义Gazebo数据集上训练。

Result: 在18,017样本的域内测试集上：位置准确率98.23%、方向准确率98.00%、完整位姿准确率96.75%；平均位置误差0.11m，平均方向误差5.7度；单样本推理速度0.62s。在7个未见物体类别上位置准确率90.99%；不完整地图微调后恢复至93.72%。消融实验显示多模态互补性：无LiDAR时95.06%，无可见物体时LiDAR维持92.33%，二者皆无时仅70.74%。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型能够有效将机器人定位重构为语义推理任务，具备跨模态鲁棒性、对未见类别的泛化能力以及对不完整地图的适应性，LiDAR在视觉语义缺失时可提供可靠的定位信号，多模态融合显著优于单模态方案。

Abstract: We address robot localization in GPS-denied indoor environments by reframing it as a semantic reasoning task rather than a geometric estimation problem. Motivated by how humans localize using object-level cues and labeled maps, we ask whether a vision-language model, given a front camera image, a polar LiDAR scan, and a top-down semantic grid map, can infer the robot pose. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-7B with LoRA and attach a lightweight regression head that predicts continuous pose coordinates (x, y, theta) directly from the final hidden state, bypassing text generation. Training uses a composite position-and-direction loss with curriculum learning on a custom Gazebo dataset of 120,112 samples and 527 scenes. On the in-distribution test set of 18,017 samples, the model achieves 98.23 percent position accuracy, 98.00 percent direction accuracy, 96.75 percent full pose accuracy, a mean position error of 0.11 m, and a mean orientation error of 5.7 degrees at 0.62 s per sample. Position accuracy drops by only 7.2 percentage points on seven unseen object categories, reaching 90.99 percent, supporting semantic spatial reasoning rather than appearance memorization. With incomplete maps, fine-tuning recovers performance to 93.72 percent position accuracy, showing adaptability to stale or partial map information. Two ablations highlight cross-modal complementarity. Without LiDAR, using only camera and map inputs, position accuracy remains 95.06 percent, only 3.2 percentage points below the full system. However, when the camera sees no visible objects in a wall-facing view, LiDAR sustains 92.33 percent position accuracy, compared with 70.74 percent when neither LiDAR nor visible objects are available. This shows that LiDAR becomes the primary localization signal when camera semantics are unavailable and provides a reliable fallback under occlusion or sparse layouts.

</details>


### [251] [ROSA: A Robotics Foundation Model Serving System for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01088)
*Wenqi Jiang,Jason Clemons,Rowland O'Flaherty,Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Shuran Song,Yashraj Narang,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSA 是一个面向机器人工厂的 RFM 服务系统，采用共享 GPU 池、机器人感知编程抽象和工厂目标驱动调度架构，相比传统专用服务系统可提升工厂生产力达 12.06 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RFM 服务系统基于单机器人-单模型假设，将推理视为边缘计算问题，导致 GPU 利用率低、机器人电池续航差，无法满足工厂多模型流水线和性能要求的场景。

Method: ROSA 基于三大设计原则：1) 共享 GPU 池服务，多机器人通过网络访问服务器级 GPU；2) 机器人感知编程抽象与系统设计，支持多模型流水线、任务级性能需求和故障处理；3) 工厂目标驱动调度，以最大化满足 SLO 的工厂生产力为目标。

Result: 基于 Ray Serve 分布式编排，集成 vLLM、PyTorch、JAX 作为模型服务后端，在真实机器人和大规模合成负载上验证，ROSA 相比传统专用服务系统提升工厂生产力最高达 12.06 倍。

Conclusion: ROSA 通过打破单机器人-单模型假设，采用共享 GPU 池和工厂目标驱动调度，显著提升机器人工厂的推理性能、GPU 利用率和整体生产力。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models (RFMs) are making general-purpose robots increasingly practical for factory deployments. While RFM serving systems are central to this vision, existing systems are largely shaped by a single-robot, single-model assumption: inference is treated as an edge-computing problem handled by an on-robot or dedicated nearby GPU, and the serving objective is to minimize the latency of a single action model. In this paper, we propose ROSA, an RFM serving system for robot factories designed around three key principles. First, ROSA adopts shared GPU-pool serving, allowing a fleet of robots to access powerful server-class GPUs over the network in order to improve inference performance, battery duration, and GPU utilization. Second, ROSA provides a robotics-aware programming abstraction and system design that supports multi-model pipelines, per-task performance requirements, and failure handling. Third, ROSA uses factory-objective-driven scheduling to maximize SLO-qualified factory productivity rather than minimizing individual request latency. We implement ROSA on top of Ray Serve for distributed orchestration, with vLLM, PyTorch, and JAX as model-serving backends, and evaluate it on both real robots and synthetic large-scale workloads. The results show that ROSA improves factory productivity by up to 12.06x over conventional dedicated serving systems.

</details>


### [252] [Technical Report: Asynchronous Distributed Trajectory Estimation of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01106)
*Adam Pooley,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分布式轨迹估计的异步块坐标下降算法，该算法通过近似最大后验估计问题，在引入可忽略误差的同时减少最多96.9%的智能体间通信，并证明迭代指数收敛到最优估计。仿真表明误差比同类最先进算法低64%，实验验证了对跨三个数量级延迟的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式轨迹估计实现通常未考虑智能体间通信和计算的异步性，这限制了其在现实机器人系统中的适用性。

Method: 提出异步块坐标下降算法，通过近似最大后验估计问题来减少通信，并从理论上证明迭代的指数收敛性。

Result: 近似引入的误差可忽略，减少了96.9%的智能体间通信；仿真显示比同类最先进算法低64%的误差；实验证明对跨越三个数量级延迟的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该异步方法有效解决了分布式轨迹估计中的异步通信与计算问题，在降低通信开销的同时保持高精度和收敛保证，并对大范围延迟具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distributed trajectory estimation arises in many applications across robotics, but existing implementations typically do not consider asynchrony in agents' communications and computations. Therefore, we propose an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm for distributed trajectory estimation. We consider a team of agents that observes a team of robots and estimates their states over a sliding window. The agents solve an approximation of the maximum a posteriori estimation problem, which we derive. We show this approximation introduces negligible errors and eliminates up to 96.9% of communications among agents. Next, we prove that agents' iterates converge exponentially fast to the optimal estimate of the robots' states. Simulations show that this approach has up to 64% less error than a comparable state-of-the-art algorithm. Experiments on mobile robots show the robustness of this approach to delays whose lengths span three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [253] [FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01111)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Failure-Aware Retry (FAR)框架，使机器人能在测试时从失败中学习，通过失败对比偏好适应、轻量级动作扰动以及将成功恢复轨迹纳入训练循环，自主完成任务。实验表明FAR在仿真和真实世界任务中分别比标准扩散策略平均提升17.6%和11.7%的成功率，并显著提高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略在真实环境部署时不可避免会遇到失败，而简单重试往往重复相同错误，现有恢复方法又大多依赖人工干预。

Method: FAR框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 失败对比偏好适应，利用失败数据构建偏好学习数据，引导策略远离先前失败行为；(2) 重试时引入轻量级动作扰动，鼓励局部探索；(3) 将成功恢复轨迹纳入训练循环，实现持续策略改进。

Result: 在仿真环境中比标准扩散策略平均提升17.6%成功率，在真实世界任务中平均提升11.7%。在持续策略改进过程中，通过利用有信息的失败案例，在重置预算和时间步预算下均显著提高数据效率。

Conclusion: FAR框架能有效使机器人从测试时的失败中自主学习并恢复，无需人工干预即可提升任务完成率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robot policies inevitably encounter failures when deployed in real environments. Naive retries often repeat the same mistakes, while many existing recovery methods rely on human intervention. In this paper, we propose Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework that enables robots to learn from previous failures at test time, adapt their behavior accordingly, and eventually complete the task autonomously. FAR combines Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation, which constructs preference learning data from failures to steer the policy away from previously unsuccessful behaviors, with lightweight action perturbations during retries to encourage local exploration. We further incorporate successful recovery trajectories into a training loop for continual policy improvement. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks show that FAR substantially improves success rates and robustness, with average gains of 17.6% over the standard diffusion policy in simulation and 11.7% in the real world. In addition, FAR significantly improves data efficiency under both reset and timestep budgets during continual policy improvement by exploiting informative failure cases.

</details>


### [254] [Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01166)
*Zhiyi Li,Peilin Wu,Xiaoshen Han,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化4D潜在预测模型，通过学习场景3D结构在潜空间中的时序演化，实现3D一致性的未来场景预测与机器人任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频预测模型在2D视频序列上操作，缺乏3D几何理解能力，导致空间推理和物理一致性不足。

Method: 构建结构化4D潜在预测模型，在潜空间中预测场景3D结构随时间的演变，可解码为多种3D格式；结合目标条件逆动力学模块将生成未来场景转化为可执行动作。

Result: 生成的未来场景具有强视觉质量，3D一致性和多视图连贯性显著优于基于视频的规划器；在复杂操作任务上表现优越，对新颖视觉条件泛化能力强，在真实机器人平台上有效。

Conclusion: 结构化4D潜在预测模型作为规划器，通过3D一致的场景理解提升了机器人任务规划的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Video predictive models are emerging as a powerful paradigm in robotics, offering a promising path toward task generalization, long-horizon planning, and flexible decision-making. However, prevailing approaches often operate on 2D video sequences, inherently lacking the 3D geometric understanding necessary for precise spatial reasoning and physical consistency. We introduce a Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model, which predicts the evolution of a scene's 3D structure in a structured latent space conditioned on observations and textual instructions. Our representation encodes the scene holistically and can be decoded into diverse 3D formats, enabling a more complete and 3D consistent scene understanding. This structured 4D latent predictive model serves as a planner, generating future scenes that are translated into executable actions by a goal-conditioned inverse dynamics module. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates futures with strong visual quality, substantially better 3D consistency and multi-view coherence compared to state-of-the-art video-based planners. Consequently, our full planning pipeline achieves superior performance on complex manipulation tasks, exhibits robust generalization to novel visual conditions, and proves effective on real-world robotic platforms. Our website is available at https://structured-4d-model.github.io/.

</details>


### [255] [FastBridge: Closing the Model-Based Realization Gap in Safety Filters on 3D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01200)
*Tscholl Dario,Nakka Yashwanth Kumar,Gunter Brian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的非线性、执行器感知的安全滤波器，利用完整四旋翼动力学和碰撞锥指数势垒函数，在紧贴机载计算限制下实现快速避障。与现有方法相比，轨迹急动度降低47%，运行速度提升2.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS安全滤波器使用单积分器或双积分器等降阶模型，忽略执行器限制，假设加速度指令可瞬时实现，导致实际飞行中安全性不足。

Method: 基于3DGS的解析碰撞锥势垒，推导高相对度的碰撞锥指数势垒函数（CBF）和备份CBF；通过前向模拟备份策略保持QP可行性，利用完整四旋翼动力学强制执行执行器感知的安全滤波器。

Result: 与现有最优3DGS安全滤波器相比，轨迹急动度降低47%，运行速度提升2.25倍；在仿真和硬件实验中均验证了实时导航性能。

Conclusion: 提出的执行器感知安全滤波器能够更有效地在紧计算约束下实现四旋翼快速飞行中的安全避障，兼顾了动力学完整性和实时性。

Abstract: Fast quadrotor flight requires safe obstacle avoidance under tight onboard compute limits. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides a continuous, geometry-aware scene representation for perception-driven navigation, existing 3DGS safety filters use reduced-order models such as single- and double-integrators that ignore actuator limits and assume commanded accelerations are realized instantaneously. Building on an analytic collision cone barrier for 3DGS, we introduce a nonlinear, actuator-aware safety filter enforced through the full quadrotor dynamics. We derive a high-relative-degree collision cone exponential CBF and a backup CBF that preserves QP feasibility under input constraints using a forward-simulated backup policy. Compared with a state-of-the-art 3DGS safety filter, our approach reduces trajectory jerk by 47% and runs 2.25 times faster. We validate the method in simulation and on hardware for real-time navigation in cluttered, perception-derived environments.

</details>


### [256] [Sensorless Four-Channel Control Architecture Using Inverse Dynamics Modeling for Human-Scale Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01201)
*Amir Noohian,Dylan Miller,Justin Valentine,Alan Lynch,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道遥操作架构，通过消除对外部力/力矩传感器的依赖，在人体尺度遥操作中实现了优于传统双通道、四通道及透明度增强方法的位置/力跟踪性能，并在全身接触的门开门案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四通道遥操作架构在人体尺度应用中受限于高惯性、建模困难以及对噪声大且昂贵的力/力矩传感器的依赖，亟需一种无传感器的解决方案来提升透明度与实用性。

Method: 提出基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道架构，在定制的WAM双臂遥操作平台上实现并验证该控制器。

Result: 该方案在位置跟踪、力跟踪、降低操作者负担及最大可传递阻抗方面均优于传统双通道、四通道及其他透明度增强方法；门开门案例中全身持续接触操作验证了其在真实人体尺度任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于逆动力学的无传感器四通道架构可有效替代依赖力传感器的传统方案，在人体尺度遥操作中实现更优的透明度和操作性能。

Abstract: The four-channel teleoperation architecture is a well-established framework for achieving transparency in bilateral systems. However, its performance in human-scale teleoperation is limited by high inertia, modeling challenges, and reliance on noisy and costly force/torque sensors. This paper introduces a sensorless four-channel architecture based on inverse dynamics modeling. The controller is implemented and validated on a customized WAM bilateral teleoperation setup. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional two- and four-channel schemes as well as transparency-enhancement methods, improving position and force tracking, reducing operator effort, and increasing maximum transmittable impedance without external sensors. A door-opening case study involving sustained whole-body contact along the manipulator further demonstrates the effectiveness of the method in realistic human-scale manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [257] [FurnitureVLA: Learning Long-Horizon Bimanual Furniture Assembly with Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01212)
*Chenyang Ma,Yue Yang,Radu Corcodel,Siddarth Jain,Andrew Wu,Chiori Hori,Diego Romeres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FurnitureVLA，首个使用视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）进行真实尺度双臂家具组装的系统性研究。通过可扩展的仿真流程、VR遥操作数据收集、以及联合预测动作和连续进度信号的增强型VLA，在三种家具类型上将平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，并成功在真实Kinova Gen3平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人家具组装研究大多局限于玩具级场景或单臂操作，缺乏对真实尺度双臂家具组装的系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，将VLA模型应用于复杂长时序的真实家具组装任务。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了真实尺度双臂家具组装任务；2) 开发了可扩展的仿真管线，用于专家数据生成和评估；3) 构建了单操作员双臂控制的VR遥操作系统，收集高质量真实演示；4) 提出进度增强型VLA（Progress-Enhanced VLA），在语义定义的子任务上微调，联合预测动作和连续进度信号，实现自动子任务切换并减少推理时的累积误差；5) 系统研究了影响真实尺度组装精度的感知和控制设计因素。

Result: 在三种家具类型上，FurnitureVLA将平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%；设计因素研究额外带来21%的性能提升。在真实Kinova Gen3平台上，最难任务的性能下降仅16%。

Conclusion: FurnitureVLA证明了VLA模型在真实尺度双臂家具组装中的有效性，进度增强机制和设计因素研究为长时序、高精度操作任务提供了可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Current work on robot furniture assembly mostly focuses on toy-scale settings or single-arm manipulation. We introduce FurnitureVLA, the first systematic study of real-scale bimanual furniture assembly using Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). We formalize the task, develop a scalable simulation pipeline for expert data generation and evaluation, and build a VR teleoperation system for single-operator bimanual control to collect high-quality real-world demonstrations. To address extreme long-horizon assembly with up to 7 subtasks and 1550 control steps, we propose a progress-enhanced VLA, finetuned on semantically grounded subtasks, that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal, enabling automatic subtask transitions and reducing compounding errors during inference. We further study perception and control design factors that critically affect precision in real-scale assembly. FurnitureVLA improves average simulation success from 48% to 80% compared to baselines across three furniture types, with an additional 21% gain from our design factor study. We validate on a real Kinova Gen3 platform with only 16% drop on the hardest task.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Comparing the Emotional Impact of Thematic Versus Episodic Framing in Visualization Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00103)
*Poorna Talkad Sukumar,Maurizio Porfiri,Oded Nov*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过一项800人参与的预注册在线实验，研究了数据可视化中文本框架（ episodic framing（事件框架） vs thematic framing（主题框架））对观众情绪反应的影响。结果表明，事件框架相比主题框架引发了更强烈的负面情绪，而注释的添加并未改变情绪影响。虽然框架未直接影响政策态度，但负面情绪的增加间接增强了对枪支管控的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知数据可视化中的文本框架会影响理解、记忆和偏见感知，但其对观众情绪反应的影响尚缺乏研究。

Method: 预注册的组间在线实验（N=800），被试观看相同美国大规模枪击数据可视化图表，仅文本框架变化（主题标题、主题标题+注释、事件标题+注释）。

Result: 事件框架引发显著更强的负面情绪；注释添加不改变情绪影响；框架不直接影响政策态度，但通过负面情绪间接增强对枪支管控的支持。

Conclusion: 情绪是文本框架影响数据可视化反应的关键但未被充分研究的维度。

Abstract: Although textual framing in data visualizations is known to influence comprehension, recall, and perceptions of bias, its effects on viewers' emotional responses remain underexplored. Drawing on two widely studied framing strategies in political communication, we examine how episodic framing (foregrounding a specific event) versus thematic framing (foregrounding broader trends) affects emotional and attitudinal responses to visualizations. We conducted a preregistered, between-subjects online experiment (N = 800) in which participants viewed identical visualizations of U.S. mass shooting data that varied only in textual framing: a thematic title, a thematic title with annotation, or an episodic title paired with the same annotation. Results show that episodic framing elicited significantly more negative emotional valence than both thematic conditions. In contrast, adding an annotation to a thematic title did not alter emotional impact. While framing did not significantly affect policy attitudes, mediation analysis revealed a significant indirect effect: increased negative emotion under episodic framing predicted greater support for gun control. These findings position emotion as a critical, yet underexamined, dimension of how textual framing shapes responses to data visualizations.

</details>


### [259] [May (A)I Beautify Your Visualization? Expert Judgments of Acceptable Aesthetic Alterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00239)
*Kalina Borkiewicz,Jixian Li,Joshua A. Levine,Katherine E. Isaacs*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过95名专家的调查，研究了3D自然现象可视化中人为和AI做出的呈现层与数据层修改的可接受性差异。结果显示：修改的可接受性主要取决于修改本身的意义（而非修改层面）；某些修改普遍被认为更可接受；AI做出的修改始终比人类做出的相同修改评分更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI等工具使3D自然现象可视化的视觉设计与修改越来越便捷，理解这些修改（尤其是AI参与时）对数据感知的影响及权衡变得日益重要。

Method: 对95名可视化研究人员、从业者和领域科学家进行专家调查，考察他们对15种修改（包括呈现层调整如光照、相机位置，以及数据层修改如去除错误、填充空白）的反应，这些修改分别由人类和AI系统实施。

Result: 1) 修改的可接受性差异主要由修改本身的意义驱动，而非操作层面（呈现层或数据层）；2) 某些修改相对于其他修改始终被认为更可接受，无论作者是人类还是AI；3) 这种相对排序大体稳定，但AI生成的修改始终比相同的人类修改获得更低的可接受性评分。

Conclusion: 存在"更可接受"与"更敏感"的修改类型之分；无论是人工设计者还是AI辅助可视化工具，都应融入反映这些差异的约束和保护机制。

Abstract: In 3D visualizations of natural phenomena, improving aesthetics can provide measurable benefits, but often involves transformations that affect how the data is perceived. As a growing range of tools - including AI-based methods - make visual design and modification more accessible, it is increasingly important to understand trade offs and concerns when making these changes. We conducted an expert survey (N=95) with visualization researchers, practitioners, and domain scientists, investigating reactions to fifteen alterations spanning presentation-level adjustments (e.g., lighting, camera position) and data-level modifications (e.g., removing errors, filling gaps), applied by both humans and AI systems. Results show differences in perceived acceptability are driven by the transformation's meaning, regardless of whether it operates at the presentation or data level. Additionally, certain modifications were consistently judged as more permissible than others regardless of human or AI authorship. While this relative ordering remains largely stable, AI-generated transformations are consistently rated as less acceptable than identical human-produced changes. These results reveal a distinction between more permissible and more sensitive alterations, and suggest the need for both designers and AI-assisted visualization tools to incorporate constraints and guardrails that reflect these differences.

</details>


### [260] [Child Safety in Generative AI: An Expert-Guided and Incident-Grounded Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00395)
*Haein Kong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对儿童安全的生成式AI风险评估框架，整合专家指导的风险因素和真实AI事故数据，并在教育领域评估了Llama Guard模型，发现其难以识别教育相关的不安全用户提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估框架主要针对一般用户群体，忽略了儿童和青少年特有的风险。随着生成式AI在儿童中的使用日益增加，亟需专门针对儿童伤害的风险评估框架。

Method: 提出了一个整合专家指导的风险因素和真实AI事故数据的儿童安全评估框架。该框架从专家指南和AI事故数据库中识别危害类别，并利用这些信息构建合成测试集用于模型评估。具体应用于教育领域，评估了三个Llama Guard模型检测不安全用户提示的能力。

Result: 当前Llama Guard模型难以识别教育相关的不安全用户提示。

Conclusion: 讨论了未来工作如何将评估扩展到更多风险类别，并让领域专家参与整个评估流程。

Abstract: As generative AI is increasingly used by children and adolescents, there is a growing need for risk evaluation frameworks that account for child-specific harms. However, most existing safety evaluation frameworks focus on general user populations, often overlooking risks unique to younger users. To address this gap, we propose an evaluation framework that integrates expert-guided risk factors with real-world AI incident data for child safety. The framework identifies hazard categories from expert guidelines and AI incident databases and uses this information to construct a synthetic test set for model evaluation. Particularly, we apply the framework to the education domain and evaluate three Llama Guard models on their ability to detect unsafe user prompts. Our results show that current Llama Guard models struggle to identify education-related unsafe user prompts. We conclude by discussing how future work can extend the evaluation to additional risk categories and incorporate domain experts throughout the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [261] [A Penny for Your Prompts: Experiments Detecting and Mitigating LLM Usage by Survey Respondents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00403)
*Zane Xu,Nathan Malkin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究量化了众包平台中LLM辅助回答的普遍性（从Prolific的<10%到MTurk的>80%），发现缓解措施可减少LLM使用但未必提升数据质量，并提出通过键盘记录和AI针对性问题来检测LLM使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台上参与者越来越多地使用大语言模型（LLM）来回答调查问卷，这可能破坏收集数据的有效性，因此需要量化这一行为的普遍性并探索检测和预防方法。

Method: 通过系列调查（N=250），考察了平台选择、问卷长度、禁止使用AI的请求以及禁用复制粘贴功能等条件，分析了LLM辅助回答的特征及其在不同条件下的频率变化，并进行了检测实验。

Result: LLM辅助回答的频率在不同平台上差异巨大（Prolific低于10%，MTurk超过80%）；缓解措施减少了LLM使用但未必然提升数据质量；当时未发现参与者使用浏览器代理；提出了基于键盘记录和AI针对性问题的检测方法。

Conclusion: 建议研究人员通过记录和分析键盘数据以及设计针对AI的指令和问题，主动筛查调查回复中的LLM使用情况。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used by participants on crowdsourcing platforms when responding to surveys, potentially undermining the validity of collected data. Our study aims to quantify the prevalence of this behavior and investigate methods to detect and prevent it. In a series of surveys (N = 250), we examined conditions such as platform choice, survey length, requests not to use AI, and disabling copy-paste functionality. We were able to identify distinct characteristics of LLM-assisted responses and found that their frequency varied widely, from under 10% on Prolific to over 80% on Mechanical Turk. Mitigation measures reduced LLM usage but did not necessarily improve data quality. No participants employed browser-use agents at the time of our survey, but we report on our own detection experiments. We recommend that researchers actively screen survey responses for LLM usage by recording and analyzing keystroke data and crafting instructions and questions aimed at AI.

</details>


### [262] [A Simple Solution to Improving Human Supervision of Algorithms: Evidence from Smart Vending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00420)
*Minda Zhao,Brian Rongqing Han,Xin Chen,Tao Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过随机实地实验（553名工人，超59,000台机器）提出了一种约束性覆盖策略——限制每次决策中的人工干预次数（每台机器最多两次向下覆盖），相比自由覆盖（库存降1.95%但销售跌1.19%）和无覆盖，约束覆盖在不损害销售的情况下降低库存1.28%，且工人能更优选择覆盖SKU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI系统进行运营决策（如库存补货）时，完全授予人类覆盖权会因人类偏见和噪声导致性能下降，而完全禁止则会忽视有价值的私有信息。因此需要回答：如何设计覆盖权限结构以改善人类对自主AI的监督？

Method: 提出约束性覆盖策略，限制每个决策时段内的覆盖次数以实现选择性过滤，优先高价值覆盖。通过在中国某大型智能售货机零售商（管理超59,000台机器、4,000个SKU）开展的随机实地实验进行验证，553名工人被随机分配至无覆盖、自由覆盖或每台机器最多两次向下覆盖三种条件。

Result: 自由覆盖使库存降低1.95%但销售下降1.19%；约束覆盖使库存降低1.28%且不损害销售，工人选择了更好的SKU进行覆盖（通过局部平均处理效应证实）。收益在经验丰富的工人、高激励SKU和成长期SKU中最大。模拟的个性化策略可进一步提高销售概率9.1%。

Conclusion: 学术界获得关于人机监督中自由裁量权设计因果效应的新见解，强调选择性过滤可提升决策质量。管理者可低成本规模化应用此政策于零售、物流、资源规划等运营场景，在不损失销售的情况下减少过剩库存，同时利用人类私有信息，无需算法重新设计、信息定制或额外培训。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) systems for operational decisions, such as inventory replenishment. Yet fully granting override rights can degrade performance due to human bias and noise, while prohibiting them may overlook valuable private information. This raises a key question: How should override rights be structured to improve human supervision of autonomous AI? Methodology/results: We propose a constrained override policy that limits overrides per decision episode to enable selective filtering that prioritizes high-value overrides. We tested it through a randomized field experiment with 553 workers at a major Chinese smart vending machine retailer that manages more than 59,000 machines and 4,000 SKUs. Workers were assigned to no overrides, free overrides, or a two-per-machine limit on downward overrides. Free overrides reduce inventory by 1.95% but also cut sales by 1.19%. Constrained overrides reduce inventory by 1.28% without harming sales, as workers select better SKUs to override, confirmed via local average treatment effects. Gains are largest for experienced workers, high-incentive SKUs, and growth-stage SKUs. A simulated personalized policy further increases sales probability by 9.1%. Managerial implications: Academics gain novel insights from the causal effects of discretion design in human-supervised AI, emphasizing selective filtering to enhance decision quality. Managers can benefit from a scalable, low-cost policy for operations such as retail, logistics, and resource planning, reducing excess inventory without sales loss while harnessing private human information, with no need for algorithmic redesign, information customization, or additional training.

</details>


### [263] [Gaze-Informed Proactive AI Assistance for Children's Picture Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00445)
*Zekun Wu,Man Su,Huiyong Li,Tomohiro Nagashima,Anna Maria Feit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Ollie，一个利用儿童注视行为进行主动式AI辅助的系统，通过注视估计注意力并生成相关图片描述。实验表明，注视引导的辅助比随机辅助更能维持儿童注意力并引导其探索相关区域，儿童、家长和教师均给予积极评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式LLM辅助大多面向成年人和任务导向场景，忽视了儿童非言语行为（如注视）表达的意图与需求。本研究旨在解决儿童图片探索中的两个关键挑战：何时提供辅助以及根据儿童非言语行为提供何种辅助。

Method: 提出了Ollie系统，利用儿童注视来估计注意力、识别当前视觉焦点，并选择相关图片区域供LLM生成简短叙述描述。采用被试内设计，比较注视引导辅助与随机辅助的效果。

Result: 注视引导的辅助能使儿童更长时间保持对当前焦点的注意，更有效地引导其关注相关图片区域。儿童、家长和幼儿园教师对Ollie的评价积极，认为其比随机辅助更符合儿童兴趣。

Conclusion: 证明了注视可作为儿童主动式AI辅助的隐式输入，为未来以儿童为中心的AI系统设计提供了启示。

Abstract: Proactive assistance with large language models (LLMs) has received growing attention in the human computer interaction (HCI) community. However, most past work on proactive LLMs' assistance has focused on adult users and task-oriented settings, leaving open how such systems could support children, whose interests and needs are often expressed through gaze and other nonverbal behaviors rather than explicit requests. In this study, we focus on two key challenges of proactive assistance in children's picture exploration: when to provide assistance and what assistance to provide based on children's nonverbal behaviors. To address these challenges, we introduce Ollie, a gaze-informed proactive artificial intelligence (AI) assistant that offers short narrative descriptions based on where a child is looking. Ollie uses children's gaze to estimate their attention, identify their current visual focus, and select a related picture region for the LLM to verbally describe. In a within-subject experiment, we compared gaze-informed assistance with random assistance. Results show that gaze-informed assistance kept children's attention on their current focus for a longer period of time, and guided them more effectively to related picture regions. Children, parents, and a participating kindergarten teacher viewed Ollie positively and consider that it better matched children's interests when compared with the random assistance. This work shows the feasibility of using gaze as an implicit input for proactive AI assistance for children and provides design implications for future child-centered AI systems.

</details>


### [264] [Draped Surfaces: A Contour-Adaptive Interface Overlaid on the Physical Environment for Mixed Reality Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00518)
*SoonUk Kwon,Barrett Ens,Pourang Irani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CAMEO提出了一种基于轮廓自适应的混合现实界面，将虚拟窗口贴合到物理表面上，以减少遮挡、改善对真实物体的视觉访问和交互。两项实验表明，该方法减少了手部运动绕路，且可控的窗口变形对文本可读性无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MR工作空间采用类似驾驶舱的浮动窗口布局，导致严重遮挡，降低了用户对物理环境的感知和对真实物体的访问能力。

Method: 提出CAMEO（Contour-Adaptive Mixed Environment Overlays），一种轮廓自适应的MR界面，将虚拟窗口贴合（draping）到物理表面上。

Result: 实验一：贴合方式相比平面空中界面减少了手部运动绕路，实现了更直接的交互；实验二：可控窗口变形相比平面表面对文本可读性无显著损害。

Conclusion: CAMEO为MR工作空间提供了一种兼顾沉浸感、可读性和环境理解的新型设计范式。

Abstract: Conventional Mixed Reality (MR) workspaces are frequently organized in cockpit-like layouts, where multiple floating windows surround the user. While this configuration facilitates access to digital content, it often induces occlusion, reducing understanding of the physical environment and limiting access to real-world objects. To overcome this challenge, we present the Contour-Adaptive Mixed Environment Overlays (CAMEO), a contour-adaptive MR interface that drapes virtual windows onto physical surfaces. This design integrates digital content with nearby items, thereby improving users' visual access to background objects and supporting interaction with them. We evaluate CAMEO in two controlled studies. The first demonstrates that draping reduces hand-movement detours relative to flat mid-air surfaces, enabling more direct interaction with nearby items. The second shows that controlled window deformation does not significantly impair text legibility when compared to flat surfaces. Together, these findings contribute a novel design paradigm for MR workspaces that balances immersion, readability, and environmental understanding.

</details>


### [265] [AI, Trust, and Teaming: The Humans-as-Handlers Approach for Autonomous and Opaque AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00523)
*Nathan G. Wood*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出将自主AI系统类比为犬类等动物，主张人类应作为"处理者"而非"用户"与之协作，以明确责任归属并建立基于信任的人机团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在医疗、作战等高影响领域引发了伦理与法律挑战，现有"用户/部署者"框架无法清晰界定人类责任，需要新的人机关系模型。

Method: 通过构建机器-动物类比，将人类角色重新定位为"处理者"（handler），分析类比的适用点与不适用点，并剥离不恰当的关系元素。

Result: 确立了"人类作为处理者"的初始框架，阐明了人类对AI系统行为结果的可追溯责任，指出了类比中不拟合的要素并予以剥离。

Conclusion: 人机团队应朝着将AI系统视为合作者而非工具的方向发展，以共同追求复杂目标。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming ubiquitous, and across domains, increasingly autonomous systems are carrying out tasks which raise significant ethical and legal challenges which demonstrate a need for strong human-machine teams rooted in trust. In this article, I argue that within highly impactful areas (such as medicine or warfighting) there are grounds for us initially treating autonomous and opaque systems as relevantly analogous to dogs (or other animals with which we have close relationships). Under this analogy, humans making use of these systems are not to be viewed as "users" or "deployers" of these systems, but instead take the role of "handlers". This recasting of roles shifts the way we view humans, AI-enabled and autonomous systems, and the relations between them, and moreover clarifies the clear and traceable lines of responsibility humans have for the outcomes brought about when using these systems. In developing this point, I clarify that the machine-animal analogy does admit disanalogous elements, but that its touch-points ground it as a starting point. I then explore how we can divest the humans-as-handlers approach of those aspects of our relationships with animals which are unfitting for how we engage with and make use of autonomous and AI-enabled systems. I conclude by arguing that the trajectory of human-machine teamings for autonomous and AI-enabled systems should be a state where we authentically view these not as artifacts which we simply make use of, but as collaborators with which we pursue complex goals and carry out complex tasks.

</details>


### [266] [You Shall Not Pass! Where and Why Developers Draw The Line on AI Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00533)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Marco Gerosa,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对448名微软专业开发者的混合方法研究，发现开发者对AI自主性的接受度因任务和个人而异；身份定义、面向人类和设计类工作的接受度最低，而AI经验丰富和风险容忍度高的开发者接受度更高；任务问责制降低委托AI行动的意愿，任务身份降低授予AI决策自主权的意愿，而任务需求则相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI承担更多软件工作，人机努力的边界正在变化。开发者如何划定AI自主性的界线，影响着工具设计和角色分配，从而保持工作的意义性。本研究基于认知评价理论、工作设计和自动化研究，探究开发者对软件工程工作中AI自主性的接受水平。

Method: 采用混合方法，对微软448名专业开发者进行研究，收集定量和定性数据，分析不同任务和个人特征下开发者对AI自主性的接受程度。

Result: 大多数开发者接受在监督下由AI产出工作，但接受度因任务和个人差异显著。身份定义、面向人类和设计类工作的接受度最低；AI经验丰富和风险容忍度高的开发者接受度更高。任务问责制降低允许AI代表开发者行动的几率，任务身份降低授予AI决策自主权的几率，而任务需求则增加委托决策的意愿。

Conclusion: 对AI自主性的偏好反映了开发者对其工作的认知体验，为设计有意义的工作提供了重要考量。

Abstract: As AI takes on more software work, the line between human and AI effort is shifting. Where developers draw that line around AI autonomy bears on how we design tools and roles that preserve meaningful work. Drawing on cognitive appraisal theory, work design, and automation research, we conducted a mixed-methods study of 448 professional developers at Microsoft to investigate their accepted levels of AI autonomy across software engineering work. Most developers accepted AI producing work under their oversight, although accepted autonomy varied substantively across tasks and individuals. Acceptance was lowest for identity-defining, human-facing, and design-oriented work, and higher among developers with more AI experience and risk tolerance. Task accountability was associated with lower odds of allowing AI to act on developers' behalf, whereas task identity was associated with lower odds of granting AI decision-making autonomy. Task demands had the opposite effect, increasing willingness to delegate decision-making to AI. Our findings suggest that preferences for AI autonomy reflect how developers cognitively experience their work, highlighting important considerations for designing meaningful work.

</details>


### [267] [AI-Centered Grand Challenges in Visual Analytics for Healthcare: Synthesizing the VAHC 2025 Community Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00542)
*Jürgen Bernard,David Gotz,Robert S Laramee,Silvia Miksch,Gabriela Morgenshtern,Renata G. Raidou,Alessio Arleo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文总结了2025年VAHC workshop中40多位参与者围绕AI在医疗可视分析中的五大挑战主题（信任与偏见、数据与基础设施、可解释性与沟通、人机交互、模型可靠性与验证）的讨论成果，并提出了跨学科的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI、医疗健康与可视化领域的交叉迅速发展，跨学科挑战日益突出。VAHC workshop自2010年起作为连接可视化与医学信息学社区的论坛，到2025年第16届之际，需要系统整合社区集体经验，识别该领域未来应优先关注的大挑战（Grand Challenges）。

Method: 对15篇VAHC workshop录取论文进行主题编码，结合40多位参与者的结构化小组讨论（围绕三大主题：技术创新vs临床现实、以人为本与可扩展的可视化分析、从基础到可操作洞察），并辅以会后反思性分析。

Result: AI是贯穿所有讨论组的最一致关注点。提炼出五大挑战簇：（1）信任与偏见，（2）数据与基础设施，（3）可解释性与沟通，（4）人机交互，（5）模型可靠性与验证。每个挑战簇附有跨学科的未来研究方向建议。

Conclusion: 分享了这五大挑战及其研究方向，作为医疗、AI和可视化社区之间讨论与合作的起点。

Abstract: The intersection of AI, healthcare, and visualization is evolving rapidly, posing challenges that cut across disciplinary boundaries and resist easy resolution. The Visual Analytics in Healthcare workshop (VAHC), co-located every other year at the IEEE VIS conference and the AMIA (American Medical Informatics Association) annual conference, has served as a forum to connect the visualization and medical informatics community since 2010. In 2025, to celebrate the 16th edition, we used the workshop as an opportunity to consolidate the community's collective experience (and expertise) and identify Grand Challenges where the field should prioritize going forward. We combined thematic coding of the 15 accepted VAHC workshop papers with structured group discussions among more than 40 participants, organized around three major themes: "Technical innovation vs. clinical reality", "Human-centered and scalable VAHC", and "From foundations to actionable insights", followed by post-workshop reflexive analysis. Across all three groups, AI emerged as the most consistently recurring concern. In this paper, we report our AI-centered insights from the VAHC 2025 group activity, contextualize them against the broader literature along five Grand Challenges themes, and distill them into five challenge clusters, each concluded with recommendations for future research directions that cross disciplinary boundaries: (1) trust and bias, (2) data and infrastructure, (3) explainability and communication, (4) human-AI interaction, and (5) model reliability and validation. We share these challenges and their associated research directions as a starting point for discussion and collaboration across the healthcare, AI, and visualization communities. All supplemental materials are available at https://osf.io/p79uj.

</details>


### [268] [Understanding How Humans Inject Knowledge into Machine Learning Workflows through Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00969)
*Yiwen Xing,Philip Beaucamp,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Yuanzhe Jin,Saiful Khan,Gennady Andrienko,Natalia Andrienko,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对过去十年IEEE VIS会议中200余篇VIS4ML论文进行了系统综述，从ML特性、可视化、交互和动作四个维度编码分析，揭示了人类如何通过交互式可视化将知识注入ML工作流的多种路径，并基于概念模型和信息论成本效益分析解释了相关现象，为VA在ML工作流中的价值提供了明确证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ML模型大多自动学习，但ML工作流（如数据标注、特征工程、模型架构设计、超参数调优等）中仍需要大量人工输入。然而，人类如何通过交互式可视化将知识注入ML工作流这一过程的系统理解尚不充分，缺乏统一的分析框架和综述性研究。

Method: 收集了过去十年IEEE VIS会议中200余篇VIS4ML论文，开发了一套从ML特性、可视化、交互和动作四个维度进行编码的分析方案，对编码数据集进行统计分析，并采用概念模型（将VA视为模型构建）和信息论成本效益分析来解释VIS4ML现象。

Result: 识别出了人类通过交互式可视化将知识注入ML工作流的不同路径；提供了VA在ML工作流中具有价值的明确证据；所有分析结果和图表已公开。
